SciCode 验证SciCode-Verified：基准缺陷如何低估: How Benchmark Defects Underestimated the
语言模型的科学编码能力Scientific-Coding Ability of Language Models

Sihan Hu Lyuhan Huang Hefei National Laboratory College of Mechanical and Electrical Engineering University of Science and Technology of China Harbin Engineering University Hefei 230026, China Harbin 150001, China Youjin Deng Kun Chen Hefei National Laboratory Institute of Theoretical Physics University of Science and Technology of China Chinese Academy of Sciences Hefei 230026, China Beijing 100190, China Corresponding author: yjdeng@ustc.edu.cnCorresponding author: chenkun@itp.ac.cn
摘要Abstract

科学代码SciCode是语言模型科学编码能力的标准衡量标准： 需要前沿科学理论及其实施的研究级问题 工作数字代码。它是人工智能分析指数的组成部分，也是 在政府和国家实验室套件中进行长期评估。然而它的分数最近 趋于稳定：2026 年最强车型紧密聚集is the standard measure of the scientific-coding ability of language models: research-level problems that demand both frontier scientific theory and its implementation as working numerical code. It is a component of the Artificial Analysis Intelligence Index and a standing evaluation in government and national-laboratory suites. Yet its scores have recently plateaued: the strongest 2026 models cluster tightly around60%60\%子问题的准确性，以及 后继模型与其前任模型联系在一起。我们将这种停滞归因于基准测试中的缺陷 本身。针对每个问题，领域专家对所有 65 个测试问题进行审核，发现 263 个缺陷；subproblem accuracy, and a successor model ties its predecessor. We trace this stagnation to defects in the benchmark itself. A per-problem, domain-expert audit of all 65 test problems uncovers 263 defects;192其中，分布在of them, spread across91%主要的 问题，导致正确的、遵循指令的解决方案是错误的 被拒绝——通过不可复制的黄金答案、过紧的公差，或者 自相矛盾的规范。关键的是，of the main problems, cause correct, instruction-following solutions to be wrongly rejected—through non-reproducible gold answers, over-tight tolerances, or self-contradictory specifications. Critically,78%78\%这些抑制分数的缺陷 需要专门的物理或数学知识来检测，而不仅仅是文书 校对。我们纠正了每一个可确认的缺陷以生产of these score-suppressing defects require specialized physics or mathematics knowledge to detect, not mere clerical proofreading. We corrected every confirmable defect to produceSciCode 验证SciCode-Verified。更正内容添加 仅适用于适定问题所需的规范，修复分级并加强测试 太宽松了；每项变更均被独立记录并附有其合理性 由第二位领域专家重新检查。我们重新评估了修正后的 12 个前沿模型快照 基准测试并找到实质性恢复：子问题精度从. The corrections add only the specifications a well-posed problem requires, repair grading, and tighten the tests that were too lenient; every change is recorded with its justification and independently re-checked by a second domain expert. We re-evaluate twelve frontier model snapshots on the corrected benchmark and find a substantial recovery: subproblem accuracy rises from4545–60%60\% to 8484–98%98\%，以及主要问题的准确度, and main-problem accuracy from99–27%27\% to 6969–92%92\%。最先进的 模型在科学编码方面比. State-of-the-art models are far more proficient in scientific coding than科学代码SciCode已建议—— 瓶颈不是模型能力，而是评估工具的质量。我们发布has suggested—the bottleneck was not model capability, but the quality of the evaluation instrument. We releaseSciCode 验证SciCode-Verified以其完整的审计线索作为纠正的公众 标准。with its complete audit trail as the corrected public standard.111项目存储库：Project repository:https://github.com/flyingwagner/scicode-verified

1 简介Introduction

科学代码SciCode (Tian et al., 2024)是衡量科学编码能力的标准工具 的语言模型。其 80 个研究级问题是 来自数学、物理、化学、生物学和材料科学的真实研究， 每个模型都将研究工作流程分解为累积的子问题：模型必须吸收 专家级科学背景，进行推导，选择合适的数值 方法，并将其实施到研究级精度。随着语言模型越来越多地应用于 从事前沿科学研究——文献综合、推导、模拟、 实验设计——对具有这种能力的仪器的需求也随之增长。is the standard instrument for measuring the scientific-coding ability of language models. Its 80 research-level problems are drawn from real research across mathematics, physics, chemistry, biology, and materials science, and each one decomposes a research workflow into cumulative subproblems: the model must absorb expert-level scientific background, carry a derivation through, choose an appropriate numerical method, and implement it to research-grade accuracy. As language models are increasingly put to work on frontier scientific research—literature synthesis, derivation, simulation, and experiment design—the need for an instrument of exactly this capability grows with it.科学代码SciCode在广泛跟踪的基准测试中占据独特的地位：编码评估测试软件 工程、科学评估测试问答，以及occupies a distinctive niche among widely tracked benchmarks: coding evaluations test software engineering, science evaluations test question answering, and科学代码SciCode同时要求两者， 真正的研究问题，其解决方案是有效的数字代码，并且已被采用 随后：demands both at once, on genuine research problems whose solutions are working numerical code—and adoption has followed:科学代码SciCode是人工智能分析指数的组成部分is a component of the Artificial Analysis Intelligence Index(Artificial Analysis, 2026a), 其公共排行榜对 280 个模型配置进行了评分, whose public leaderboard scores 280 model configurations against it(Artificial Analysis, 2026c); 政府评估人员将其纳入英国人工智能安全研究所的检查评估中 套房; government evaluators include it in the UK AI Security Institute’s Inspect Evals suite(UK AI Security Institute, 2026);和国家实验室将其作为常设机构运行 评价; and national laboratories run it as a standing evaluation(Lawrence Berkeley National Laboratory, 2026)。最近的模型卡和技术报告也越来越多 评估. Recent model cards and technical reports likewise increasingly evaluate on科学代码SciCode旁边既定的 基准alongside established benchmarks(Google DeepMind, 2026; Team et al., 2026; 2025; OpenAI, 2026; ByteDance Seed, 2026; Artificial Analysis, 2026b).

然而，对于这种地位的乐器来说，它的乐谱表现得很奇怪。最能说明问题的是， 前沿在数字上无法区分：在公共排行榜上，2026 年最强车型 彼此之间的距离不超过四点——《克劳德寓言 5》 最强公共模型 2026 年中期整体智力指数得分For an instrument of this standing, however, its scores behave strangely. Most tellingly, the frontier is numerically indistinguishable: on the public leaderboard, the strongest 2026 models sit within four points of each other—Claude Fable 5, the strongest public model of mid-2026 on the overall Intelligence Index, scores60%60\%; Gemini 3.1 Pro和Kimi K3评分; Gemini 3.1 Pro and Kimi K3 score59%59\%; GPT-5.6 Sol 与其前身 GPT-5.5 并列; GPT-5.6 Sol ties its predecessor GPT-5.5 at56%56\% (Artificial Analysis, 2026c).222人工分析Artificial Analysis科学代码SciCode排行榜leaderboard(Artificial Analysis, 2026c), 访问时间：2026 年 7 月 21 日：克劳德寓言 5, accessed 2026-07-21: Claude Fable 560%60\%, 双子座 3.1 专业版, Gemini 3.1 Pro59%59\%, 基米K3, Kimi K359%59\%、缪斯火花1.1, Muse Spark 1.158%58\%;接下来的九个前沿条目分数; the next nine frontier entries score5353–56%56\%，其中GPT-5.5及其后继者 GPT-5.6 Sol，并列于, among them GPT-5.5 and its successor GPT-5.6 Sol, tied at56%56\%;列出的 280 种配置（每个型号有多个设置）的平均值; the 280 listed configurations (multiple settings per model) averaged42.9%42.9\%在 2026 年 6 月 2 日快照中。in the 2026-06-02 snapshot.无法区分旗舰产品的仪器 它的前身只为其留下了两种解释 低分：要么科学编码确实超出了当今的模型，要么 仪器本身校准错误。An instrument that cannot tell a flagship from its predecessor leaves only two explanations for its low scores: either scientific coding genuinely lies beyond today’s models, or the instrument itself is miscalibrated.

我们发现大部分差距源于基准测试的设计及其缺陷，而不是来自 模型。我们的领域专家对所有 65 个测试问题的审查发现了 263 个缺陷。我们修复了所有问题，只有一个We find that much of the gap stems from the benchmark’s design and its defects, not from the models. Our domain-expert review of all 65 test problems finds 263 defects. We fix all of them but one333我们无法解决的问题是问题 2 （The one we cannot fix is problem 2 (Gaussian_Beam_Focus) 本身：它的规范 没有修复唯一的答案，因此不承认没有可验证的黄金，所以我们放弃这个问题而不是 运送任意黄金（§) itself: its specification fixes no unique answer and so admits no verifiable gold, so we drop the problem rather than ship an arbitrary gold (§2).到 生产to produceSciCode 验证SciCode-Verified，随本文发布的修正基准。的两个属性, the corrected benchmark released with this paper. Two properties of科学代码SciCode的 设计使缺陷成本高昂：子问题形成累积链——步骤’s design make defects costly here: subproblems form a cumulative chain—stepkk建立在步骤上builds on steps1:k−11{:}k{-}1——因此，早期步骤的缺陷会污染下游的每一步；得分是 全有或全无，因此一个有缺陷的子问题就放弃了主要问题。已确认的缺陷密集且大多是科学的：—so a defective early step contaminates every step downstream; and scoring is all-or-nothing, so one defective subproblem forfeits the main problem. The confirmed defects are dense and mostly scientific:192导致正确的、遵循说明的解决方案被评分错误——通过不可重现或 不正确的黄金答案、过紧的公差、过度指定的随机性或不完整和 自相矛盾的规格和触摸cause a correct, instruction-following solution to be graded wrong—through non-reproducible or incorrect gold answers, over-tight tolerances, over-specified randomness, or incomplete and self-contradictory specifications—and touch91%主要问题（58 64），同时of the main problems (58 of 64), while192 中的 150150 of the 192(78%) 只有专家才能识别出缺陷 相关物理或数学知识。由于每个模型都遇到相同的损坏步骤，因此所有模型共享的缺陷就变成了 所有模型共享的上限——这正是在模型上观察到的压缩和停滞 排行榜。后面的更正(78%) are recognizable as defects only with specialist knowledge of the relevant physics or mathematics. Since every model meets the same broken steps, a defect shared by all models becomes a ceiling shared by all models—which is precisely the compression and stagnation observed on the leaderboard. The corrections behindSciCode 验证SciCode-Verified保持最小：每个都提供一个约束 一个适定的问题需要但提示是隐式的，或者修复评分，并且测试太 宽松，我们收紧了它，所以没有纠正的问题承认错误的答案（§stay minimal: each either supplies a constraint that a well-posed problem requires but the prompt left implicit, or repairs grading, and where a test was too lenient we tightened it, so no corrected problem admits a wrong answer (§4）。 每项变更均记录其合理性并进行独立重新检查（§). Every change is recorded with its justification and independently re-checked (§A).

固定Fixing科学代码SciCode很难有一个特定的原因：它的大部分缺陷是科学的而不是 规范中的文书原则层面的差距与黄金价值的数值错误一样多 和公差——因此识别和纠正它们都需要领域专家。错误的金值或公差不必通过任何表面检查，并且 认识到它——更不用说计算正确的——意味着重做物理问题， 问题，跨越五个研究领域。因此，我们的审计是在科学层面上进行的 而不是代码：独立审阅者重新得出每个问题的目标并进行压力测试 它的规范，以及第二次对抗性传递重新验证了每个标记的缺陷以消除 误报。批准的更改及其理由都会被记录下来，并且每个版本都会根据该记录进行机械检查（§is hard for a specific reason: most of its defects are scientific rather than clerical—principle-level gaps in the specification as much as numerical errors in gold values and tolerances—so both recognizing and correcting them demands domain specialists. A wrong gold value or tolerance need not fail any surface check, and recognizing it—let alone computing the right one—means redoing the physics, problem by problem, across five research fields. Our audit therefore worked at the level of the science rather than the code: an independent reviewer re-derived each problem’s targets and stress-tested its specification, and a second, adversarial pass re-verified every flagged defect to eliminate false alarms. Approved changes are recorded with their justification, and each release is mechanically checked against that record (§A）。结果，). The result,SciCode 验证SciCode-Verified，现在可以服务, can now serve科学代码SciCode的最初目的： 判断模型对前沿科学理论及其实现代码的真正掌握程度。’s original purpose: to judge models’ true command of frontier scientific theory and of the code that implements it.

2 背景：Background:科学代码SciCode及其评估协议and its evaluation protocol

科学代码SciCode (Tian et al., 2024)包含 80 个主要问题，分为 15 个开发和 65个测试主要问题。船舶公开的15个发展问题 用于开发和少量使用的真实代码，因此测量是我们的，就像所有已发布的一样 结果——使用 65 个问题的测试分割。每个子问题 附带一个 Python 函数头、指定输入/输出的文档字符串、可选的专家编写的 候选解决方案必须通过的背景和领域测试用例。子问题解决了contains 80 main problems, split into 15 development and 65 test main problems. The 15 development problems ship public ground-truth code for development and few-shot use, so measurement—ours, as in all published results—uses the 65-problem test split. Each subproblem ships a Python function header, a docstring specifying inputs/outputs, optional expert-written background, and domain test cases the candidate solution must pass. Subproblems are solved累积地cumulatively：步骤: stepkk可能会调用黄金（参考） 步骤的功能may call the gold (reference) functions of steps1:k−11{:}k{-}1。仅当满足以下条件时，主要问题才算已解决：. A main problem counts as solved only if全部all它的子问题 通过——一种全有或全无的规则，在该规则下，单个不良黄金价值或过于严格的容忍度会失败 整个问题。我们将此报告为its subproblems pass—an all-or-nothing rule under which a single bad gold value or over-tight tolerance fails the entire problem. We report this as主要问题main-problem准确性和使用accuracy, and use步骤step和and子问题subproblem可以互换。interchangeably.

域测试用例可能会通过两种方式错误地使正确的解决方案失败。一、预先计算的gold 回答它 比较本身可能是错误的，或者无法通过任何方法重现 - 因此任何正确的输出都不一致 与它。二、比较（数值A domain test case can wrongly fail a correct solution in two ways. First, the precomputed gold answer it compares against may itself be wrong, or reproducible by no method—so any correct output disagrees with it. Second, the comparison (a numerical全部关闭allclose）可能太严格：公差 比方法所保证的更严格，或者默默地要求约定提示从未修复 （符号、单位、索引原点、网格或随机数顺序）。无论哪种方式，一个正确的， 遵循说明的解决方案可能会被评分错误。) may be too strict: a tolerance tighter than the method warrants, or one that silently demands a convention the prompt never fixed (a sign, a unit, an index origin, a grid, or a random-number order). Either way, a correct, instruction-following solution is liable to be graded wrong.

我们审核所有 65 个测试问题。其中之一，问题 2 （We audit all 65 test problems. One of them, problem 2 (Gaussian_Beam_Focus），在结构上是不确定的：它的规范没有修复 独特的答案，其黄金是无法复制的。与我们遇到的所有其他缺陷不同， 它无法纠正为可验证的问题，因此我们放弃它而不是任意发送 黄金。 （因此，已发布的 65 个问题分数已经包含了一个问题， 没有正确的解决方案可以通过。）), is structurally underdetermined: its specification fixes no unique answer, and its gold is reproducible by no method. Unlike every other defect we encounter, it cannot be corrected into a verifiable problem, so we drop it rather than ship an arbitrary gold. (Published 65-problem scores therefore already include one problem that no correct solution can pass.)

3 重新评估Re-evaluation onSciCode 验证SciCode-Verified

OnSciCode 验证SciCode-Verified，范围上升到, the ranges rise to84–98%和and69–92%，分别 （表, respectively (Table1;图; Figure2）。每个模型都有所收获). Every model gains2929–4040子问题 点和subproblem points and5353–7070主要问题点。纠正分布在大多数地区的子问题缺陷 问题消除了以前导致整个主要问题被评分为的错误故障 不正确，从而在主要问题的准确性方面产生更大的收益。我们分析了患病率和 这些缺陷的影响§main-problem points. Correcting subproblem defects distributed across most problems removes false failures that previously caused entire main problems to be scored as incorrect, producing the larger gains in main-problem accuracy. We analyze the prevalence and effects of these defects in §4.

在上限，修正后的基准接近饱和：GPT-5.6 Sol 达到At the upper end, the corrected benchmark approaches saturation: GPT-5.6 Sol reaches98.3%98.3\%子问题和subproblem and92.2%92.2\%主要问题的准确性。然而，在整个模型集中，校正确实 不会统一改变所有分数：它既扩大了之前压缩的差距，又改变了模型 排名。在原始基准测试中，GPT-5.5 只通过了两个主要问题： DeepSeek V4 Pro 预览版；上main-problem accuracy. Across the full model set, however, correction does not shift all scores uniformly: it both widens previously compressed gaps and changes model rankings. On the original benchmark, GPT-5.5 passes only two more main problems than DeepSeek V4 Pro Preview; onSciCode 验证SciCode-Verified，又过了八个。排名也发生变化：Seed 2.1 Pro 排名 第一是原始主要问题的准确性，但第六是, it passes eight more. Rankings also change: Seed 2.1 Pro ranks first by original main-problem accuracy but sixth onSciCode 验证SciCode-Verified。完整的模式显示在 图. The full pattern is shown in Figure2和表and Table1.

关注人工分析排行榜Following the Artificial Analysis leaderboard(Artificial Analysis, 2026c)，我们报告 pass@1。除了 两个校正基准 DeepSeek V4 预览行，平均运行 3 次，每个条目都是一个 跑。 Pass@1 分数因运行而异，因此模型之间的差异很小，特别是在 主要问题的准确性，不应过度解释。, we report pass@1. Except for the two corrected-benchmark DeepSeek V4 Preview rows, which average three runs, each entry is a single run. Pass@1 scores vary across runs, so small differences between models, particularly in main-problem accuracy, should not be over-interpreted.

没有背景。Without the background.

科学代码SciCode最真实的设定保留了 专家撰写的背景。有了它，GPT-5.5、Gemini 3.5 Flash 和 Claude Opus 4.8 解决了’s most realistic setting withholds the expert-written background. With it, GPT-5.5, Gemini 3.5 Flash, and Claude Opus 4.8 solve92–97%的子问题数of subproblems onSciCode 验证SciCode-Verified，该指标接近饱和。没有它， 他们的子问题精度下降到, approaching saturation on this metric. Without it, their subproblem accuracy falls to80–83%及其主要问题的准确性and their main-problem accuracy to52–66%（表(Table2）。因此，虽然 with-background 基准几乎没有留下什么 由于子问题准确性的空间，无背景设置保留了有意义的余量。我们分析 § 中这三个模型的无失败/有通过行为). Thus, while the with-background benchmark leaves little room on subproblem accuracy, the no-background setting retains meaningful headroom. We analyze the fail-without/pass-with behavior of these three models in §3.1.

3.1 背景提供什么知识What knowledge the background supplies

为了了解背景的贡献，我们分析了 31 个子问题，其中至少有两个 三个模型——GPT-5.5、Gemini 3.5 Flash 和 Claude Opus 4.8——在没有背景的情况下失败了，但 与它一起过去。为了确定根本原因，我们比较了配对的几代人，重现了 计算，并将下游故障追溯到其原始步骤。其中模型归因 失败后，我们确定了四个反复出现的根本原因。To understand what the background contributes, we analyze 31 subproblems that at least two of three models—GPT-5.5, Gemini 3.5 Flash, and Claude Opus 4.8—fail without the background but pass with it. To identify root causes, we compare the paired generations, reproduce the computations, and trace downstream failures to their originating step. Among model-attributable failures, we identify four recurring root causes.

(i) 重新组装多步推导。(i) Reassembling a multi-step derivation.在#32（光学结合纳米颗粒阵列）中， 背景从光学结合力走向声子哈密顿量——陷阱刚度In #32 (optically bound nanoparticle arrays), the background walks from the optical-binding force to the phonon Hamiltonian—trap stiffnesski=α​Ei2/w2k_{i}=\alpha E_{i}^{2}/w^{2}，结合位移频率, binding-shifted frequenciesΩi=(ki+∑jki​j)/m\Omega_{i}=\sqrt{(k_{i}+\sum_{j}k_{ij})/m}, 签名跳频, signed hoppinggi​j=−ki​j/(2​m​Ωi​Ωj)g_{ij}=-k_{ij}/(2m\sqrt{\Omega_{i}\Omega_{j}})。如果没有它，这三个模型将产生 三. Without it, the three models produce three互相矛盾mutually inconsistent哈密顿主义者：Claude Opus 4.8 忽略了光阱刚度， GPT-5.5 省略了结合引起的频移，Gemini 3.5 Flash 将陷阱加倍 刚度。在这里，背景提供了模型无法可靠地提供的衍生支架 自己重建。Hamiltonians: Claude Opus 4.8 omits the optical-trap stiffness, GPT-5.5 omits the binding-induced frequency shift, and Gemini 3.5 Flash doubles the trap stiffness. Here, the background provides derivational scaffolding that the models do not reliably reconstruct on their own.

(ii) 教科书先验推翻了规定的表述。(ii) Textbook priors overriding the stated formulation.在#54（SUPG 有限元）中， 问题陈述明确指定了一个非标准的弱形式，但所有三个模型都默默地 将其替换为In #54 (SUPG finite elements), the problem statement explicitly specifies a non-standard weak form, yet all three models silently replace it with the课本textbookSUPG/Nitsche 配方。重述方程中的 实施时的背景使模型保持在所述变体上，这表明 强大的教科书先验会覆盖提示中已经存在的信息。SUPG/Nitsche formulation. Restating the equations in the background at the point of implementation keeps the models on the stated variant, suggesting that a strong textbook prior overrides information already present in the prompt.

(iii) 专业领域事实。(iii) Specialized domain facts.在#69（层状电子气的拉曼响应）中， 模型得到了一个特定领域的事实错误：反向散射光子动量转移是In #69 (Raman response of a layered electron gas), the models get a domain-specific fact wrong: the backscattering photon momentum transfer is2​k2k, 不, notkk.

(iv) 未遂执行错误。(iv) Near-miss implementation errors.在#79（Nosé–Hoover 链）中，模型使用 纠正可逆积分因式分解，但会出现孤立的簿记错误，例如减半 半步两次或重复使用陈旧的力阵。这里后台作为实现 检查而不是提供缺少的方法。In #79 (Nosé–Hoover chains), the models use the correct reversible-integrator factorization but make isolated bookkeeping errors, such as halving a half-step twice or reusing a stale force array. Here, the background acts as an implementation check rather than supplying a missing method.

与这些是知识差距而不是噪音相一致，无背景惩罚更大 对于 DeepSeek V4 预览版对（表Consistent with these being knowledge gaps rather than noise, the no-background penalty is larger for the DeepSeek V4 Preview pair (Table2）：他们输了): they lose2121–2222子问题点， 与相比subproblem points, compared with1010–1717对于此诊断中分析的三个模型。for the three models analyzed in this diagnostic.

库版本稳健的分级。Library-version-robust grading.

我们在两个固定的 NumPy/SciPy 环境中对每个保存的解决方案进行评分，如果符合，则将其视为正确 要么通过。这样就恢复了We grade each saved solution in two pinned NumPy/SciPy environments and count it as correct if it passes either. This recovers77–99每个模型有 287 个子问题，否则失败只是因为 库版本不兼容；不同模型的救援集几乎相同 （附录of 287 subproblems per model that otherwise fail only because of library-version incompatibilities; the rescued sets are nearly identical across models (AppendixB）。因此，该规则减少了环境引起的漏报 不偏袒任何模型。). The rule therefore reduces environment-induced false negatives without favoring any model.

科学的失效机制。Scientific failure mechanisms.

177 个科学缺陷分为七个亚型 （表The 177 scientific defects fall into seven subtypes (Table4）。最大的是未指定的约定 (77)，其中黄金固定 提示留下了自由的选择，然后是错误的黄金（33）和依赖于 RNG 的分级（22）。 下面的七个例子展示了每种子类型如何扭曲评估。). The largest is unspecified conventions (77), where the gold fixes a choice that the prompt leaves free, followed by wrong gold (33) and RNG-dependent grading (22). The seven examples below show how each subtype distorts evaluation.

  • •

    未指定约定，#74。Unspecified convention, #74.户主二维码Householder QR(Householder, 1958)连续应用反射器来消除对角线以下的条目。在最后一栏中， 一个successively applies reflectors to eliminate entries below the diagonal. In the final column, a1×11\times 1反射器不会消除任何内容：应用它只会翻转最后一列的符号 的reflector eliminates nothing: applying it only flips the sign of the last column ofQQ和最后一个对角线条目and the last diagonal entry ofRR，留下产品, leaving the productQ​RQR不变。应用这个 因此，反射器和在其之前停止都是有效的约定。提示没有 指定使用哪一个，而gold只接受前者；我们现在声明该公约 明确地。unchanged. Applying this reflector and stopping before it are therefore both valid conventions. The prompt did not specify which to use, while the gold accepted only the former; we now state that convention explicitly.

  • •

    错误的黄金（符号错误），#22。Wrong gold (sign error), #22.旋转系数递推The rotation-coefficient recurrence(Gumerov and Duraiswami, 2004)发货时出现符号错误（shipped a sign error (−2→+2-2\!\to\!+2）使得传输矩阵变得非酉。修正后的 符号将唯一的酉维格纳系数再现为) that makes the transfer matrix non-unitary. The corrected sign reproduces the unique unitary Wigner coefficient to10−1410^{-14}.

  • •

    取决于 RNG 的分级，#68。RNG-dependent grading, #68.原始扩散蒙特卡罗 测试The original diffusion Monte-Carlo test(Metropolis et al., 1953; Reynolds et al., 1982)取决于特定的随机数序列。 相反，我们检查最终统计数据，要求平均能量在depended on a particular random-number sequence. We instead check the final statistic, requiring the mean energy to be within0.050.05哈的 氦基态能量。Ha of the helium ground-state energy.

  • •

    非歧视性测试，#5.1。Non-discriminating test, #5.1.第一次 Lanczos 测试The first Lanczos test(Lanczos, 1950)将反射对称三对角矩阵与回文起始向量配对。因为 矩阵保持反射对称性，所有 Krylov 向量保持四维对称 子空间，尽管测试需要六个 Lanczos 向量。因此迭代必须分解 在执行所有要求的步骤之前。我们用不对称向量替换起始向量；它的 前六个 Krylov 向量是线性独立的，因此现在使用相同的测试来执行完整的运行。paired a reflection-symmetric tridiagonal matrix with a palindromic starting vector. Because the matrix preserves reflection symmetry, all Krylov vectors remain in the four-dimensional symmetric subspace, although the test requests six Lanczos vectors. The iteration must therefore break down before exercising all requested steps. We replace the starting vector with an asymmetric one; its first six Krylov vectors are linearly independent, so the same test now exercises the full run.

  • •

    公差过紧，#30/#67。Over-tight tolerance, #30/#67.对于氦气 Slater-Jastrow 波函数For the helium Slater–Jastrow wavefunction(Jastrow, 1955)和层状电子气and the layered electron gas(Jain and Allen, 1985), 一个, an全部关闭allclose at rtol=10−15\texttt{rtol}=10^{-15}（下面 双精度）或四舍五入指纹失败了每个正确的实现。我们 最小程度地放松以承认合法的方法/舍入选择，同时仍然拒绝错误 答案。(below double precision) or against a rounding fingerprint fails every correct implementation. We loosen minimally to admit the legitimate method/rounding choice while still rejecting wrong answers.

  • •

    规格Spec↔\leftrightarrow黄金矛盾，#8。gold contradiction, #8.对于傅里叶平面空间 过滤For Fourier-plane spatial filtering(Goodman, 1996)，提示需要严格的高通边界，但是金 使用包容性 (, the prompt requires a strict high-pass boundary, but the gold uses an inclusive (≥\geq）边界。我们重新生成黄金以符合提示。) boundary. We regenerate the gold to match the prompt.

  • •

    无效或不可重现的目标，#28.1。Invalid or non-reproducible target, #28.1.对于高斯光束传播 透镜系统For Gaussian-beam propagation through a lens system(Kogelnik and Li, 1966)，目标无法通过标准方法重现，并且 与后面的子问题相矛盾。我们用提示规定的方法重新生成它。, the target is reproducible by no standard method and contradicts a later subproblem. We regenerate it with the prompt-prescribed method.

修正仍然存在困难。The corrections preserve difficulty.

为了解决这些缺陷，我们添加了必要的 惯例，纠正错误的黄金答案，并采用更合适的验证标准。 这些变化可以防止有效的解决方案被错误评分，从而使模型获得更高的分数 而不降低问题的科学难度。To address these defects, we added necessary conventions, corrected erroneous gold answers, and adopted more appropriate verification criteria. These changes prevent valid solutions from being misgraded, allowing models to achieve higher scores without reducing the scientific difficulty of the problems.

5 相关工作Related work

基准验证工作已经解决了多种缺陷。Benchmark-verification work has addressed several kinds of defects.UT升压UTBoost (Yu et al., 2025)强化过度宽容strengthens overly permissiveSWE-长凳SWE-bench (Jimenez et al., 2024)针对错误补丁进行测试， 同时tests against erroneous patches, whileSWE-bench 已验证SWE-bench Verified (OpenAI, 2024)人工验证 500 个任务子集。其他 工作纠正了抑制准确性的缺陷：事实一致性中的标签错误 基准human-validates a 500-task subset. Other work corrects defects that suppress accuracy: label errors in factual-consistency benchmarks(Nahum et al., 2025), 错误的金币, wrong golds in人类评估HumanEval (Liu et al., 2023)，以及缺陷 数据工程任务, and defects in data-engineering tasks(Zanoli et al., 2026). MMLU-Redux (Gema et al., 2025)和白金 基准测试and Platinum Benchmarks(Vendrow et al., 2025)进一步表明残余标签噪声接近饱和。further show that residual label noise matters near saturation.科学代码SciCode包含两种问题：一些测试太弱，而黄金值不正确，过于严格 公差和未指定的数值约定拒绝有效的解决方案。contains both kinds of problems: some tests are too weak, while incorrect gold values, over-tight tolerances, and unspecified numerical conventions reject valid solutions.

SciCode 的问题跟踪器记录了类似的评分缺陷，包括过于严格 公差SciCode’s issue tracker documents similar grading defects, including over-strict tolerances444https://github.com/scicode-bench/SciCode/issues/8和黄金解决方案 他们自己的测试失败。and gold solutions that fail their own tests.555https://github.com/scicode-bench/SciCode/issues/43

6 局限性Limitations

开发拆分发布了黄金答案和真实代码，因此不用于 模型评估。我们的审核和更正仅涵盖用于评估的 65 个问题测试分组； 所有 263 个已识别的缺陷均来自此拆分。 Pass@1 分数表现出运行与运行之间的差异。因为 许多型号已经接近饱和The development split releases gold answers and ground-truth code and is therefore not used for model evaluation. Our audit and corrections cover only the 65-problem test split used for evaluation; all 263 identified defects come from this split. Pass@1 scores exhibit run-to-run variation. Because many models are already near saturation onSciCode 验证SciCode-Verified，小分数之间的差异更大 对这种变化很敏感。我们报告的原始基准的子问题精度也不同 与人工分析排行榜上的有些不同, small score differences among them are more sensitive to this variation. Our reported subproblem accuracies on the original benchmark also differ somewhat from those on the Artificial Analysis leaderboard(Artificial Analysis, 2026c)。 尽管审计经历了多轮审查，但所有审查均由作者进行 因此不构成外部盲验证。. Although the audit underwent multiple rounds of review, all reviews were conducted by the authors and therefore do not constitute external blind validation.

7 结论Conclusion

科学代码SciCode区分前沿模型的能力受到基准测试缺陷的限制 本身。我们对所有 65 个测试问题的审核发现了 263 个缺陷；我们纠正每一个可修复的 缺陷并排除一个无法验证的问题。重新评估十二个前沿 模型快照’s ability to distinguish frontier models has been limited by defects in the benchmark itself. Our audit of all 65 test problems identifies 263 defects; we correct every repairable defect and exclude one problem that cannot be made verifiable. Re-evaluating twelve frontier model snapshots onSciCode 验证SciCode-Verified，我们发现子问题的准确度从, we find that subproblem accuracy rises from4545–60%60\% to 8484–98%98\%和 主要问题准确度来自and main-problem accuracy from99–27%27\% to 6969–92%92\%。我们发布修正后的基准 连同其缺陷和纠正的完整记录，以支持公平和可重复 科学编码能力评估。. We release the corrected benchmark together with a complete record of its defects and corrections to support fair and reproducible evaluation of scientific-coding ability.

SciCode 验证SciCode-Verified评估单一问题的科学编码而不是长期代理工作流程 涉及工具使用、环境交互、迭代开发。开发可靠 此类工作流程的基准仍然是未来工作的重要方向。evaluates single-problem scientific coding rather than long-horizon agentic workflows involving tool use, environment interaction, and iterative development. Developing reliable benchmarks for such workflows remains an important direction for future work.

附录AAppendix A审核和纠正工作流程Audit and correction workflow

我们一次解决一个测试问题。对于每一个，我们都针对其参考解决方案进行了运行 自己的测试用例，标记了我们发现的每一个缺陷——错误的或不可复制的黄金、过紧的 容差、RNG 相关分级、未指定的约定或截断的 或自相矛盾的提示 - 并应用有针对性的修复，重新生成受影响的黄金目标 当仅靠文本编辑无法解决问题时。每个更改都被记录，每个更正的参考 解决方案在发布前通过了自己的（更正的）测试，以及发布的数据集和评估 文件是直接从这些每个问题的更正中重建的。We went through the test problems one at a time. For each, we ran its reference solution against its own test cases, marked every defect we found—a wrong or non-reproducible gold, an over-tight tolerance, RNG-dependent grading, an unspecified convention, or a truncated or self-contradictory prompt—and applied a targeted fix, regenerating the affected gold targets when a text edit alone could not resolve it. Every change is logged, every corrected reference solution passes its own (corrected) tests before release, and the released dataset and evaluation files are rebuilt directly from these per-problem corrections.

审计以连续的审查方式进行，每轮都按照审计的结构进行The audit ran as successive review rounds, each structured as audit→\to修复fix→\to对抗性的 重新审查adversarial re-review→\to释放门。每个批准的变更都会被记录（问题、字段、之前、 之后，基本原理，回合）并应用于单个每个问题的源文件，从中发布 文件被重新生成。在每次发布之前，自动检查都会验证两个方向 - 每个 记录的更改出现在版本中，每个更改的字段都有记录支持并重新运行 黄金自检。然后，每个修复都面临独立的对抗性重新审查，必须重现 缺陷并试图反驳修复。在无法确认的情况下，修复被推翻并 重做。问题 13 是一个已记录的示例：奇偶校验修复触发了重新审核和测试 重新设计跨越六个下游子问题。release gate. Each approved change is recorded (problem, field, before, after, rationale, round) and applied to a single per-problem source file, from which the released files are regenerated. Before each release, an automated check verifies both directions—every recorded change appears in the release, and every changed field is backed by a record—and re-runs the gold self-tests. Every fix then faced an independent adversarial re-review that had to reproduce the defect and attempt to refute the fix. Where it could not confirm, fixes were overturned and redone. Problem 13 is a documented example: a parity fix triggered a re-audit and a test redesign spanning six downstream subproblems.

两项披露。首先，重新生成黄金目标改变了一些预先计算的值：69 目标群体与上游版本不同。其中 64 个对应于已记录的更改； 其余五个（子问题 1.1、61.1、69.5、73.1、80.6）仅因为字节相同而改变 黄金代码在现代软件环境中产生不同的值。在这五个中的四个上，所有 四个模型具有匹配的运行前/运行后（§Two disclosures. First, regenerating the gold targets changed some precomputed values: 69 target groups differ from the upstream release. 64 of these correspond to recorded changes; the remaining five (subproblems 1.1, 61.1, 69.5, 73.1, 80.6) changed only because byte-identical gold code yields different values in a modern software environment. On four of those five, all four models with matched before/after runs (§3) 从不及格翻转到通过；上游预先计算的值变得无法访问。 这五个是) flip from fail to pass; the upstream precomputed values had become unreachable. These five are不not计入 262 个缺陷。二、对每个问题进行审核 由一位领域专家进行，每个批准的修复程序都会在一秒钟内重新检查。因为第二次通过 确认而不是独立注释，我们报告没有注释者之间的协议 （§counted among the 262 defects. Second, each problem was audited by one domain expert and every approved fix re-checked by a second. Because that second pass confirms rather than independently annotates, we report no inter-annotator agreement (§6).

Second, main-problem effects differ across models only because of independent failures elsewhere in the same problems: Gemini 3.5 Flash, for example, gains no main problem despite seven rescued steps.

问题5——Problem 5 —兰佐斯Lanczos 数学、数值线性代数Mathematics, Numerical Linear Algebra（1 个缺陷）(1 defect)

原始来源：Original source: (Lanczos, 1950)

  • •

    5.1  科学：非歧视性测试（物理）scientific: Non-discriminating test (physics)  ⋅\cdot太宽松（测试收紧）too-lenient (test tightened)
    原始起始向量 b 是回文且近对称的，因此与对称三对角 A 配对，它会产生一个退化的 Krylov 子空间，有缺陷的 Lanczos 实现可以通过该子空间，并产生模糊的黄金目标。修正后的 b 打破了对称性，确保测试执行真正的非简并 Lanczos 运行。The original starting vector b is palindromic and near-symmetric, so paired with a symmetric tridiagonal A it produces a degenerate Krylov subspace that a flawed Lanczos implementation can pass and that yields an ambiguous gold target. The corrected b breaks the symmetry, ensuring the test exercises a genuine non-degenerate Lanczos run.

问题8——Problem 8 —空间过滤器_IIISpatial_filters_III 物理、光学Physics, Optics（4个缺陷）(4 defects)

原始来源：Original source: (Goodman, 1996)

  • •

    8.1（问题描述主要+子步骤[0].步骤描述提示）8.1 (problem_description_main + sub_steps[0].step_description_prompt)  表面：截断/乱码/丢弃常数surface: Truncation / garble / dropped constant  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    问题描述包含从兄弟问题复制的描述低通行为（“仅通过衍射图案的中心最大值”）的句子，直接与该任务的十字形高通滤波器目标相矛盾，并误导任何读取规范的模型。The problem description contains a sentence describing low-pass behavior (‘pass only the central maximum of the diffraction pattern’) copied from a sibling problem, directly contradicting this task’s cross-shaped high-pass filter objective and misleading any model that reads the spec.

  • •

    8.1（sub_steps[0].function_header 文档字符串）8.1 (sub_steps[0].function_header docstring)  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    文档字符串省略了匹配 gold 输出所需的两个事实：T 是零频率中心 (fftshift) 布局中的二进制 {0,1} 掩码，filtered_image 是逆变换的实部 - 因此返回幅度的正确解决方案或非中心掩码将被错误地拒绝。The docstring omits two facts required to match the gold output: that T is a binary {0,1} mask in the zero-frequency-centered (fftshift) layout, and that filtered_image is the real part of the inverse transform—so a correct solution returning the magnitude or a non-centered mask would be incorrectly rejected.

  • •

    8.1（问题_io）8.1 (problem_io)  表面：错别字/格式surface: Typo / formatting  ⋅\cdot中性清理neutral-cleanup
    I/O 块包含两个拼写错误（“bandwitdh”、“Ouput”），并将第二个输出命名为“output_image”而不是“filtered_image”，从而与函数头不一致；这些是表面文本修正，对评分没有影响。The I/O block contains two spelling errors (‘bandwitdh’, ‘Ouput’) and names the second output ‘output_image’ instead of ‘filtered_image’, creating an inconsistency with the function header; these are surface text corrections with no effect on grading.

  • •

    8.1（目标/黄金）8.1 (target / gold)  科学：规格scientific: Spec↔\leftrightarrow黄金矛盾gold contradiction  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    原始的黄金掩模使用包含（>=）边界，保留|kx|=带宽和|ky|=带宽交叉线，同时提示明确指出“滤波器掩模不应包括带宽频率”；使用严格“>”排除的正确解决方案在大约 0.45% 的边界像素上失败，因此使用严格“>”重新生成黄金以匹配规定的规格。The original gold mask used an inclusive (>=) boundary, keeping the |kx|=bandwidth and |ky|=bandwidth cross-lines, while the prompt explicitly states ‘the filter masks should not include the bandwidth frequency’; a correct solution using strict ‘>’ exclusion failed on the ~0.45% of boundary pixels, so the gold was regenerated with strict ‘>’ to match the stated spec.

问题9——Problem 9 —加权_雅可比Weighted_Jacobi 数学、数值线性代数Mathematics, Numerical Linear Algebra（1 个缺陷）(1 defect)

原始来源：Original source: (Briggs et al., 2000)

  • •

    9.1  科学：错误的黄金（方法/符号/对称性）scientific: Wrong gold (method / sign / symmetry)  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    该规范的更新公式除以 (a_ii * omega^-1)，但省略 (1-omega) x_i^(k) 阻尼项，使其仅在 omega=1 时正确，并在 omega=2/3 和 omega=0.5 时产生错误的迭代。修正后的公式为标准阻尼雅可比：x_i^(k+1) = (1-omega) x_i^(k) + omega * (b_i - sum_{j!=i} a_ij x_j^(k)) / a_ii。The spec’s update formula divides by (a_ii * omega^-1) but omits the (1-omega) x_i^(k) damping term, making it correct only at omega=1 and producing wrong iterates for omega=2/3 and omega=0.5. The corrected formula is the standard damped Jacobi: x_i^(k+1) = (1-omega) x_i^(k) + omega * (b_i - sum_{j!=i} a_ij x_j^(k)) / a_ii.

问题11——Problem 11 —GADC_纠缠GADC_entanglement 物理学、量子信息/计算Physics, Quantum Information/Computing（5个缺陷）(5 defects)

原始来源：Original source: (Chen et al., 2024)

  • •

    问题_描述_主要；步骤11.6；步骤11.7；步骤11.11；步骤 11.12（步骤描述提示）problem_description_main; step 11.6; step 11.7; step 11.11; step 11.12 (step_description_prompt)  表面：截断/乱码/丢弃常数surface: Truncation / garble / dropped constant  ⋅\cdot中性清理neutral-cleanup
    数据集摄取在五个描述字段中丢弃了标记，产生了不语法的片段（“用 and 函数编写一个函数”、“接收器函数”、“在 . 中进行测量”、单粒子扇区标签中缺少“1.”）；更正可以在不改变任务的情况下恢复散文语法。Dataset ingestion dropped tokens across five description fields, producing ungrammatical fragments (‘Write a function with and functions’, ‘receiver function’, ‘measurement in .’, missing ‘1.’ in the one-particle sector label); the corrections restore grammatical prose without changing the task.

  • •

    步骤11.1（ket）；步骤11.3（张量）function_headerstep 11.1 (ket); step 11.3 (tensor) function_header  表面：接口/退货合同不匹配surface: Interface / return-contract mismatch  ⋅\cdot中性清理neutral-cleanup
    函数头“def ket(dim)”和“def tensor()”省略了它们自己的文档字符串描述的参数（“args”表示 ket，“*args”表示张量），从而使签名无法按照指定调用；当向量的克罗内克积是 nd 数组时，张量的输出类型也被错误地表述为“2d 数组”。The function headers ‘def ket(dim)’ and ‘def tensor()’ omit parameters their own docstrings describe (‘args’ for ket, ‘*args’ for tensor), making the signatures uncallable as specified; tensor’s output type was also mis-stated as ‘2d array’ when the Kronecker product of vectors is an nd array.

  • •

    步骤11.4（应用通道）；步骤11.8（系统静音）；步骤 11.9 (partial_trace) function_headerstep 11.4 (apply_channel); step 11.8 (syspermute); step 11.9 (partial_trace) function_header  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot中性清理neutral-cleanup
    apply_channel、syspermute 和partial_trace 的sys 和perm 参数未指定子系统索引基数；在不知道索引是基于 0 还是基于 1 的情况下，使用相反约定的正确实现将被评为错误，因此更正始终将约定固定为基于 1。The sys and perm arguments of apply_channel, syspermute, and partial_trace leave the subsystem-index base unspecified; without knowing whether indices are 0-based or 1-based, a correct implementation using the opposite convention would be graded wrong, so the correction pins the convention to 1-based throughout.

  • •

    步骤 11.10（step_background，冯诺依曼熵）step 11.10 (step_background, von Neumann entropy)  科学：错误的黄金（方法/符号/对称性）scientific: Wrong gold (method / sign / symmetry)  ⋅\cdot中性清理neutral-cleanup
    冯·诺依曼熵规范显示公式 S = -tr(rho log_2 rho)，但随附的散文指出“In 表示（自然）矩阵对数”，与所示的 log_2 直接矛盾。遵循该散文的实现者将使用自然对数计算以 nats 为单位的熵，而遵循该公式则给出以位为单位的熵；两者相差 ln(2) ~= 0.693。更正将错误的散文标签替换为“log_2 表示以 2 为底的矩阵对数”，使规范和公式保持一致，从而使预期单位（位）明确。The von Neumann entropy spec displays the formula S = -tr(rho log_2 rho) but the accompanying prose states “In denotes the (natural) matrix logarithm,” directly contradicting the log_2 shown. An implementer following the prose would compute entropy in nats using the natural logarithm, whereas following the formula gives entropy in bits; the two differ by a factor of ln(2) ~= 0.693. The correction replaces the erroneous prose label with “log_2 denotes the base-2 matrix logarithm,” making spec and formula consistent so the intended unit (bits) is unambiguous.

  • •

    步骤11.12（step_background，测量后状态）step 11.12 (step_background, post-measurement state)  科学：错误的黄金（方法/符号/对称性）scientific: Wrong gold (method / sign / symmetry)  ⋅\cdot中性清理neutral-cleanup
    测量后状态的规范公式将分子写为 tr(Pi rho Pi)/p，其中 tr(Pi rho Pi) = p 是等于 1 的标量，因此表达式折叠为标量 1 而不是密度矩阵。正确的表达式是 Pi rho Pi / p，它将预测量状态投影到测量结果上并对其进行重新归一化。任何遵循所述公式的实现都会产生标量，从而使下游量（例如相干信息）未定义或不正确。The spec’s formula for the post-measurement state writes the numerator as tr(Pi rho Pi)/p, where tr(Pi rho Pi) = p is a scalar equal to 1, so the expression collapses to the scalar 1 rather than a density matrix. The correct expression is Pi rho Pi / p, which projects the pre-measurement state onto the measurement outcome and renormalizes it. Any implementation that follows the stated formula produces a scalar, making downstream quantities such as coherent information undefined or incorrect.

问题 12 —Problem 12 —薛定谔_DFT_with_SCFSchrodinger_DFT_with_SCF 化学、量子化学Chemistry, Quantum Chemistry（4个缺陷）(4 defects)

原始来源：Original source: (Hartree, 1928)

  • •

    12（问题_io）12 (problem_io)  表面：接口/退货合同不匹配surface: Interface / return-contract mismatch  ⋅\cdot中性清理neutral-cleanup
    问题级 I/O 文档字符串仅通过“tolerance”列出输入，并完全省略“iteration”参数，即使 scf_routine 函数签名及其测试需要显式的最大迭代计数。仅从规范实现该函数的读者无法知道该参数是否存在、其类型 (int) 或其作为 SCF 循环绑定的角色。该修复添加了缺失的“迭代：自洽字段迭代的最大数量；int”行，使规范完整并与实际接口保持一致。The problem-level I/O docstring lists inputs only through “tolerance” and omits the “iteration” parameter entirely, even though the scf_routine function signature and its tests require an explicit maximum-iteration count. A reader implementing the function from the spec alone has no way to know this argument exists, its type (int), or its role as the SCF loop bound. The fix adds the missing “iteration: the maximum number of self-consistent field iterations; int” line, making the spec complete and consistent with the actual interface.

  • •

    12.2（Numerov 测试用例）12.2 (Numerov test_cases)  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    Numerov 返回一个原始波函数，其幅度与未指定的第一步种子成线性比例，因此将原始数组与固定种子 gold 进行比较会拒绝种子不同的每个正确形状的解决方案；将种子从 -1e-10 更改为 -1e-3 可以避免 r=0 库仑/离心奇点附近的爆炸，并且通过 L2 范数对两个数组进行归一化使得测试尺度不变。Numerov returns a raw wavefunction whose amplitude scales linearly with the unspecified first-step seed, so comparing the raw array against a fixed-seed gold rejects every correct-shape solution whose seed differs; changing the seed from -1e-10 to -1e-3 avoids blow-up near the r=0 Coulomb/centrifugal singularity, and normalizing both arrays by their L2 norm makes the test scale-invariant.

  • •

    12.3（计算_施罗德步骤_描述_提示）12.3 (compute_Schrod step_description_prompt)  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot中性清理neutral-cleanup
    在积分方向之前声明的变量名称“u_at_0”和“up_at_0”被读取为r=0处的边界条件，而不是最大半径起点处的IVP种子，导致模型从错误的一端进行积分；重新排序句子以将种子显式绑定到最大半径开始可以消除歧义。The variable names ‘u_at_0’ and ‘up_at_0’, stated before the integration direction, read as boundary conditions at r=0 rather than as the IVP seed at the largest-radius starting point, causing models to integrate from the wrong end; reordering the sentence to bind the seed explicitly to the largest-radius start removes the ambiguity.

  • •

    12.14（scf_routine 提示 + test_cases）12.14 (scf_routine prompt + test_cases)  科学：无效/不可重现的目标scientific: Invalid / non-reproducible target  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    原始的 SCF 设置（混合比 0.5、平坦的 Hartree 初始猜测 (-2 + 2Z)、仅 10 次迭代和容差 1e-7）不会收敛到物理解，因此黄金（电荷密度、总能量）不收敛；该修复使用混合 0.3、物理驱动的热启动 2Z(1-exp(-2r))、60 次迭代和容差 1e-6，生成正确的求解器可以重现的聚合黄金。The original SCF setup—mixing ratio 0.5, a flat Hartree initial guess (-2 + 2Z), only 10 iterations, and tolerance 1e-7—does not converge to a physical solution, so the gold (charge_density, total_energy) is non-converged; the fix uses mixing 0.3, a physically motivated warm-start 2Z(1-exp(-2r)), 60 iterations, and tolerance 1e-6, producing a converged gold that a correct solver can reproduce.

问题 13 —Problem 13 —麦克斯韦方程求解器Maxwell_Equation_Solver 物理、光学Physics, Optics（7个缺陷）(7 defects)

原始来源：Original source: (Knapp et al., 2002)

  • •

    13.9  科学：错误的黄金（方法/符号/对称性）scientific: Wrong gold (method / sign / symmetry)  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    A_z是一个极向量，因此它在x=0和y=0内表面上的镜像宇称与A_x和A_y的镜宇称耦合；配置的 sigma=(-1,-1,+1) 对称性要求 A_z=(-,-,-)，但原始黄金存储 A_z=(+,+,-)，违反了 x 面和 y 面约束，并使目标与正确的基于物理的解决方案不相容。A_z is a polar vector, so its mirror parities on the x=0 and y=0 inner faces are coupled to those of A_x and A_y; the configuration’s sigma=(-1,-1,+1) symmetry requires A_z=(-,-,-), but the original gold stores A_z=(+,+,-), violating both the x-face and y-face constraints and making the target irreconcilable with a correct physics-based solution.

  • •

    13.10  科学：无效/不可重现的目标scientific: Invalid / non-reproducible target  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    13.10 update_fields 目标是使用错误的 A_z=(+,+,-) 奇偶校验根据 13.9 导数计算得出的，因此错误同样传播到时间步长结果中；只有 A_z 发生变化（每个测试用例精确地改变因子*dt*delta_A_z），而其他六个字段分量保持正确。The 13.10 update_fields target was computed from the 13.9 derivatives using the erroneous A_z=(+,+,-) parity, so the error propagates identically into the time-stepped result; only A_z changes (by exactly factor*dt*delta_A_z per test case) while the other six field components remain correct.

  • •

    13.11  科学：取决于 RNG 的分级scientific: RNG-dependent grading  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    原始测试通过在物理上无意义的输入（字段=（x，y，z，x，y，z，1））上运行的黄金特定ICN（迭代Crank-Nicolson）的精确字节再现来对步进器进行评分；由于 ICN 迭代计数、子步到 dt 映射和平均形式均未指定，因此任何其他有效的稳定方案在 ~1e-2 处偏离黄金，远高于分级容差，因此正确的求解器会失败。相反，修复等级是针对无散物理输入上的收敛 RK4 参考，且容差范围与该方案自身的二阶收敛层相匹配。The original test grades the stepper by exact byte-reproduction of gold’s specific ICN (iterated Crank-Nicolson) run on physically meaningless inputs (fields=(x,y,z,x,y,z,1)); because ICN iteration count, substep-to-dt mapping, and averaging form are all unspecified, any other valid stable scheme diverges from gold at ~1e-2, far above the grading tolerance, so correct solvers fail. The fix grades instead against a converged RK4 reference on divergence-free physical inputs within a tolerance matched to the scheme’s own second-order convergence floor.

  • •

    13.13  科学：取决于 RNG 的分级scientific: RNG-dependent grading  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    积分函数组成相同的未指定步进器，并通过从 Gold 的精确 ICN（迭代 Crank-Nicolson）积分中字节复制约束序列来进行分级；任何有效的稳定时间积分方案在 1e-2 到 1 级别上都会偏离黄金，这使得测试成为方案指纹检查而不是物理检查。修正后的测试将最终的 7 场状态与收敛的 RK4 参考 (atol=2e-4) 进行比较，并断言约束序列是有限且有界的。The integrate function composes the same under-specified stepper and was graded by byte-reproducing the constraint series from gold’s exact ICN (iterated Crank-Nicolson) integration; any valid stable time-integration scheme diverges from gold at the 1e-2 to 1 level, making the test a scheme-fingerprint check rather than a physics check. The corrected test compares the final 7-field state to a converged RK4 reference (atol=2e-4) and asserts the constraint series is finite and bounded.

  • •

    13.15  科学：取决于 RNG 的分级scientific: RNG-dependent grading  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    顶层主函数通过在粗网格（{10,20,40,52}中的n）上从gold的精确ICN（迭代Crank-Nicolson）积分中字节再现约束序列，将正确性耦合到任意步进方案来进行分级；任何有效的积分器都不同于 1e-2 级别的黄金积分器。校正后的测试将来自单个细网格运行 (n=64) 的约束系列与从测量的 div-E 放大下限 (~1.4e-7) 中选择的紧公差 (atol=1e-6) 的收敛 RK4 参考进行比较。The top-level main function was graded by byte-reproducing the constraint series from gold’s exact ICN (iterated Crank-Nicolson) integration at coarse grids (n in {10,20,40,52}), coupling correctness to the arbitrary stepping scheme; any valid integrator differs from gold at the 1e-2 level. The corrected test compares the constraint series from a single fine-grid run (n=64) against a converged RK4 reference with a tight tolerance (atol=1e-6) chosen from the measured div-E amplification floor (~1.4e-7).

  • •

    13.15  表面：接口/退货合同不匹配surface: Interface / return-contract mismatch  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    主要文档字符串将返回类型声明为“元组列表”，其中每个元组保存（时间、约束值），但该函数实际上返回约束值的平面一维ndarray（由Integrate生成的数组）；尽管实现了正确的物理原理，遵循文档字符串的求解器仍会发出（时间，值）对，并因形状不匹配而失败。The main docstring declares the return type as ‘list of tuples’ where each tuple holds (time, constraint value), but the function actually returns a flat 1-D ndarray of constraint values (the array produced by integrate); a solver following the docstring emits (time, value) pairs and fails on shape mismatch despite implementing the correct physics.

  • •

    13.12  科学：取决于 RNG 的分级scientific: RNG-dependent grading  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    check_constraint 计算没有方案自由度的纯散度算子 (||div E||)，但其原始测试首先在过度指定的场配置上运行 ICN（迭代 Crank-Nicolson）步进器，然后评估算子，因此分级量继承了步进器的精确再现耦合。尽管如此，使用不同（同样有效）字段演化的 check_constraint 的任何正确实现都会产生数字上不同的 ||div E||并使断言失败。该修复完全消除了这两个问题：E 直接设置为已知的无 div 偶极场（预期 ||div E|| ~ 0）和线性场 E=(x,y,z)（预期 div = 3），使检查成为确定性的、与方案无关的操作员测试 atol=1e-9。check_constraint computes a pure divergence operator (||div E||) with no scheme freedom, but its original test first ran the ICN (iterated Crank-Nicolson) stepper on an over-specified field configuration and then evaluated the operator, so the graded quantity inherited the stepper’s exact-reproduction coupling. Any correct implementation of check_constraint that nonetheless uses a different (equally valid) field evolution would produce a numerically distinct ||div E|| and fail the assertion. The fix decouples the two concerns entirely: E is set directly to a known div-free dipole field (expected ||div E|| ~ 0) and to a linear field E=(x,y,z) (expected div = 3), making the check a deterministic, scheme-independent operator test with atol=1e-9.

    14.2（problem_io/输出规范）14.2 (problem_io / output spec)  科学：规格scientific: Spec↔\leftrightarrow黄金矛盾gold contradiction  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong

    14.2（sub_steps[1].test_cases和general_tests）14.2 (sub_steps[1].test_cases and general_tests)  科学：取决于 RNG 的分级scientific: RNG-dependent grading  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong

问题 16 —Problem 16 —戴维森方法Davidson_method 数学、数值线性代数Mathematics, Numerical Linear Algebra（2个缺陷）(2 defects)

    16.2（和问题级输入/输出块）16.2 (and problem-level Inputs/Output block)  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot中性清理neutral-cleanup
    原始输出规范仅表示“计算的特征值”，未指定计数（num_eigenvalues 最低）和所需的排序（升序），因此返回不同计数或未排序特征值的正确实现将被测试错误地拒绝。The original output spec said only ‘computed eigenvalues’, leaving unspecified both the count (num_eigenvalues lowest) and the required ordering (ascending), so correct implementations returning a different count or unsorted eigenvalues would be wrongly rejected by the test.

    16.2  科学：非歧视性测试（物理）scientific: Non-discriminating test (physics)  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    由于非对角噪声接近于零（0.0 到 0.0001），矩阵几乎是对角的，导致戴维森能量校正分母为 0/0 并捕获正确的求解器；将噪声提高到 0.05 可以消除这种简并性，并且还破坏了对角排序捷径，该捷径可以通过简并情况，但无法进行真正的戴维森迭代。With near-zero off-diagonal noise (0.0 to 0.0001), the matrix is nearly diagonal, causing 0/0 in the Davidson energy-correction denominator and trapping correct solvers; raising noise to 0.05 removes this degeneracy and also breaks a diagonal-sort shortcut that passes the degenerate case but fails genuine Davidson iterations.

    18.1 / 18.2 文档字符串 + Problem_io（function_header + Problem_io 字段）18.1 / 18.2 docstrings + problem_io (function_header + problem_io fields)  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong

    18.2 步骤_背景18.2 step_background  表面：截断/乱码/丢弃常数surface: Truncation / garble / dropped constant  ⋅\cdot中性清理neutral-cleanup

    21.1（问题描述主要）+ 21.2（步骤描述提示）21.1 (problem_description_main) + 21.2 (step_description_prompt)  表面：截断/乱码/丢弃常数surface: Truncation / garble / dropped constant  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong

    24.1 测试用例24.1 test_cases  科学：非歧视性测试（物理）scientific: Non-discriminating test (physics)  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    奇数单元计数 (9, 99, 29) 将中心高斯求积节点精确地放置在 x=0 处的 IC 不连续性上，使得单元平均值取决于在跳跃点分配的分支；预先计算的目标假定 u(0)=+1，而规范将 x<=0 定义为 sin-1，给出 -4/9，因此使用该分支的任何正确求解器都会失败。即使单元计数（10、100、30 个单元，n_x=11/101/31）也会将不连续性放置在单元边界上，在 x=0 处没有正交节点，从而完全消除分支模糊性。Odd cell counts (9, 99, 29) place the central Gauss quadrature node exactly on the IC discontinuity at x=0, making the cell-averaged value depend on which branch is assigned at the jump point; the precomputed target assumed u(0)=+1 while the spec defines x<=0 as sin-1 giving -4/9, so any correct solver using that branch would fail. Even cell counts (10, 100, 30 cells, giving n_x=11/101/31) place the discontinuity on a cell boundary with no quadrature node at x=0, removing the branch ambiguity entirely.

    27.2  科学：错误的黄金（方法/符号/对称性）scientific: Wrong gold (method / sign / symmetry)  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong

问题 28 —Problem 28 —Gaussian_Beam_Intensity 物理、光学Physics, Optics（8个缺陷）(8 defects)

原始来源：Original source: (Kogelnik and Li, 1966)

  • •

    问题_io； sub_steps[1](28.2).function_header; sub_steps[2](28.3).function_headerproblem_io; sub_steps[1](28.2).function_header; sub_steps[2](28.3).function_header  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot中性清理neutral-cleanup
    最初将 z、L1 和 s 描述为“距透镜”或“距源”的相对距离（以米为单位），但黄金几何将所有三个视为沿毫米级传播轴的绝对位置（腰部 z = s，透镜 z = L1）；相对距离读数与目标不一致，因此基于其构建的解决方案被错误评分。The original described z, L1, and s as relative distances ‘from the lens’ or ‘from the source’ in meters, but the gold geometry treats all three as absolute positions along the propagation axis at millimeter scale (waist at z = s, lens at z = L1); the relative-distance reading is inconsistent with the targets, so solutions built on it were misgraded.

  • •

    sub_steps[0](28.1).step_background  科学：错误的黄金（方法/符号/对称性）scientific: Wrong gold (method / sign / symmetry)  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    背景高斯光束场表达式包含两个与标准 Kogelnik-Li 理论相矛盾的乱码：Gouy 相位项“arctan(z/f)”与括号内的指数并置，没有减法运算符，使其成为乘法因子而不是相减相位，并且焦平面处的光束腰公式读取为 2*sqrt(w0)，而不是正确的 sqrt(2)*w0。任何直接源自此背景的学生实现都会产生一个具有错误相位依赖性和不正确腰值的字段，导致基于标准理论的正确解决方案被标记为错误，而不是遵循乱码规范的代码或预期输出。The background Gaussian-beam field expression contains two garbled errors contradicting standard Kogelnik-Li theory: the Gouy phase term “arctan(z/f)” is juxtaposed with the bracketed exponent with no subtraction operator, making it a multiplicative factor rather than a subtracted phase, and the beam-waist formula at the focal planes reads 2*sqrt(w0) instead of the correct sqrt(2)*w0. Any student implementation derived directly from this background would produce a field with the wrong phase dependence and incorrect waist value, causing a correct solution based on standard theory to be marked wrong against code or expected output that follows the garbled spec.

  • •

    sub_steps[0](28.1).step_description_prompt  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    该规范指导傅里叶域传播，而不固定哪个变体；黄金仅匹配菲涅耳传递函数 H = exp(-i*pi*lambda*z*(fx^2+fy^2))，并且脉冲响应实现在第二个输出点与它相差大约 17.5%，因此正确的脉冲响应解决方案被错误地拒绝。The spec directs a Fourier-domain propagation without pinning which variant; the gold matches only the Fresnel transfer-function H = exp(-i*pi*lambda*z*(fx^2+fy^2)), and an impulse-response implementation differs from it by roughly 17.5% at the second output point, so correct impulse-response solutions were wrongly rejected.

    sub_steps[0](28.1).function_header  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong

    30.3  科学：过度严格的宽容scientific: Over-tight tolerance  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    数值拉普拉斯 RMSE 除以 delta^2，放大 value() 中的最后一位舍入，因此预先计算的目标是参考实现的舍入指纹，而不是物理上有意义的值；任何忠实但独立编码的波函数都无法通过精确的全封闭检查，即使其 RMSE 在科学上是正确的。The numerical-laplacian RMSE divides by delta^2, amplifying last-digit rounding in value(), so the precomputed target is a roundoff fingerprint of the reference implementation rather than a physically meaningful value; any faithful but independently coded wavefunction fails the exact allclose check even though its RMSE is scientifically correct.

问题 31 —Problem 31 —独立成分分析independent_component_analysis 物理学、计算物理学Physics, Computational Physics（3个缺陷）(3 defects)

    31.1  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    文档字符串未指定标准差约定，因此正确的 ddof=1 实现相对于 ddof=0 gold 被评为错误，并且与步骤 31.2 和 31.3 中使用的 ddof=1 协方差 (np.cov) 不一致。该修复始终遵循对 SD (ddof=1) 进行采样的约定。The docstring left the standard-deviation convention unspecified, so a correct ddof=1 implementation was graded wrong against ddof=0 gold, and was also inconsistent with the ddof=1 covariance (np.cov) used in steps 31.2 and 31.3. The fix pins the convention to sample SD (ddof=1) throughout.

  • •

    31.2  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    白化矩阵仅定义为正交旋转和符号，因此使用 np.allclose 将 Z 与单个预先计算的矩阵进行比较会拒绝所有其他正确的白化。该测试被规范不变检查所取代：恒等协方差加上最小二乘行空间测试，确认 Z 跨越与居中输入相同的行空间。A whitened matrix is defined only up to orthogonal rotation and sign, so comparing Z to a single precomputed matrix with np.allclose rejects all other correct whitenings. The test is replaced by a gauge-invariant check: identity covariance plus a least-squares row-space test confirming Z spans the same row space as the centered input.

  • •

    31.3  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    ICA 解决方案只能识别排列、符号和比例，因此使用 np.allclose 将输出固定到一个预先计算的矩阵会拒绝所有其他有效的分离。此外，两个测试输入（3x8 和 3x10 矩阵）太短，无法唯一分离。该修复将精确比较替换为排列和符号不变 |corr| >= 0.99 匹配器和替代可识别盲源分离混合物（锯齿波/均匀/拉普拉斯和正弦/均匀/拉普拉斯）。ICA solutions are identified only up to permutation, sign, and scale, so pinning the output with np.allclose against one precomputed matrix rejects all other valid separations. Additionally, the two test inputs (3x8 and 3x10 matrices) are too short to be uniquely separable. The fix replaces exact comparison with a permutation- and sign-invariant |corr| >= 0.99 matcher and substitutes identifiable blind source separation mixtures (sawtooth/uniform/laplace and sine/uniform/laplace).

    32.2  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong

    32.3  科学：过度严格的宽容scientific: Over-tight tolerance  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    在 Gamma=0（无衰减）情况下，总占用量完全守恒，但超过 100000 步的正确龙格-库塔积分会累积超过 1e-6 的浮点漂移；因此，原始阈值拒绝有效的求解器，并且放松到 1e-3 仍然强制守恒，同时承认合法的数值积分错误。In the Gamma=0 (no decay) case the total occupation is conserved exactly, but a correct Runge-Kutta integration over 100000 steps accumulates floating-point drift exceeding 1e-6; the original threshold therefore rejects valid solvers, and loosening to 1e-3 still enforces conservation while admitting legitimate numerical integration error.

    33.3  科学：过度严格的宽容scientific: Over-tight tolerance  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong

    37.1  科学：错误的黄金（方法/符号/对称性）scientific: Wrong gold (method / sign / symmetry)  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    近轴像距公式使用入射角 i 而不是折射角 i'，得出 l' = r*i/u' + r。物理上正确的近轴关系需要折射角i’，而式(2)中相应的封闭式表达式同样是错误的；遵循原始规范的求解器实际上会计算出黄金拒绝的错误图像距离。The paraxial image-distance formula uses the incidence angle i instead of the refraction angle i’, giving l’ = r*i/u’ + r. The physically correct paraxial relation requires the refracted angle i’, and the corresponding closed-form expression in eq (2) is wrong in the same way; a solver following the original spec literally computes a wrong image distance that the gold rejects.

  • •

    步骤 37.2 计算入射角远大于 5 度的边缘（非近轴）光线轨迹，其中小角度近似失败，但背景句子和文档字符串摘要都将其称为“近轴”。自相矛盾的标签模糊了函数模型的机制。Step 37.2 computes the marginal (non-paraxial) ray trace for incidence angles much greater than 5 degrees, where the small-angle approximation fails, yet both the background sentence and the docstring summary call it ‘paraxial’. The self-contradictory label obscures which regime the function models.

    39.2  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong

    sub_steps[0](步骤 50.1).step_description_promptsub_steps[0](step 50.1).step_description_prompt  科学：取决于 RNG 的分级scientific: RNG-dependent grading  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong

问题 52 —Problem 52 —Shooting_algo_H_atom 物理学、计算物理学Physics, Computational Physics（6个缺陷）(6 defects)

    52.1  科学：非歧视性测试（物理）scientific: Non-discriminating test (physics)  ⋅\cdot太宽松（测试收紧）too-lenient (test tightened)
    所有三个原始测试都设置 u=0，使得二阶导数项 (l(l+1)/r^2 - 2Z/r - En)*u 相同为零，无论势如何；返回 [y[1], 0] 的无操作实现可以通过所有情况。在第一个测试中设置 u0=1.0 使势项非零，并且实际上限制了径向薛定谔物理。All three original tests set u=0, making the second-derivative term (l(l+1)/r^2 - 2Z/r - En)*u identically zero regardless of the potential; a no-op implementation returning [y[1], 0] passes every case. Setting u0=1.0 in the first test makes the potential term nonzero and actually constrains the radial-Schrodinger physics.

  • •

    52.2  表面：接口/退货合同不匹配surface: Interface / return-contract mismatch  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    文档字符串将输出 ur 声明为标量浮点数，输入网格声明为“linespace”，但每个测试目标都是通过对数空间网格传递的 shape-(100,) float64 数组；遵循记录的类型和网格名称的求解器无法针对数组目标进行类型检查和 np.allclose 。The docstring declared the output ur as a scalar float and the input grid as ‘linespace’, but every test target is a shape-(100,) float64 array passed over a logspace grid; a solver following the documented type and grid name fails both type checks and np.allclose against the array target.

  • •

    52.2, 52.3  科学：无效/不可重现的目标scientific: Invalid / non-reproducible target  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    氢束缚态需要负能量；原始的正（散射）能量产生以积分器噪声为主的向内积分轮廓，因此预先计算的目标在积分器设置中是不可再现的。用负束缚态值替换 En 并缩小半径范围可产生具有一致、可再生目标的条件良好的积分。Hydrogen bound states require negative energy; the original positive (scattering) energies produce inward-integrated profiles dominated by integrator noise, so the precomputed targets are unreproducible across integrator settings. Replacing En with negative bound-state values and narrowing the radius ranges yields well-conditioned integrals with consistent, regenerable targets.

  • •

    52.4  表面：交叉引用损坏surface: Broken cross-reference  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    提示引用了具有三个参数的 Shoot(En, R, l)，但步骤 52.3 中定义的实际函数需要第四个参数 y0；调用记录的签名会引发类型错误。提示还将 l 描述为“最大”角动量，但 FindBoundStates 采用单个 l 值，并在外部处理外部循环。The prompt referenced Shoot(En, R, l) with three arguments, but the actual function defined in step 52.3 requires a fourth parameter y0; calling the documented signature raises a TypeError. The prompt also described l as a ‘maximum’ angular momentum, but FindBoundStates takes a single l value with the outer loop handled externally.

  • •

    52.4  科学：过度严格的宽容scientific: Over-tight tolerance  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    向内积分的最浅束缚态能量对约 1e-7 水平的积分器步长敏感，因此默认的 np.allclose 容差（rtol=1e-5，atol=0）可以拒绝数值正确的求解器。添加 atol=1e-5 承认这种积分器噪声分散，同时仍然拒绝物理上错误的能量。The shallowest bound-state energies from inward integration are sensitive to integrator step size at the level of ~1e-7, so the default np.allclose tolerance (rtol=1e-5, atol=0) can reject a numerically correct solver. Adding atol=1e-5 admits this integrator-noise scatter while still rejecting physically wrong energies.

  • •

    52.4  表面：轻微破损测试surface: Trivially broken test  ⋅\cdot太宽松（测试收紧）too-lenient (test tightened)
    在断言中使用 .any() 意味着只要第一个束缚态 l=0，无论其能量值如何，检查都会通过；例如，Bnd[0]=(0, 42.0) 满足原始测试。用 .all() 替换 .any() 需要角动量和能量来匹配目标，从而使测试非空的。Using .any() in the assertion means the check passes whenever the first bound state has l=0, regardless of its energy value; for example, Bnd[0]=(0, 42.0) satisfies the original test. Replacing .any() with .all() requires both the angular momentum and the energy to match the target, making the test non-vacuous.

    Problem_description_main（+ sub_steps[2] 步骤 54.3 step_description_prompt）problem_description_main (+ sub_steps[2] step 54.3 step_description_prompt)  科学：规格scientific: Spec↔\leftrightarrow黄金矛盾gold contradiction  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong

    55.1  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    原始规范仅定性地描述了分步方案，省略了显式欧拉非线性更新 u_1 = u_N + dt(eps*u_N - u_N - u_N^3)、精确的 k 空间传播器 exp[dt(2k^2/q0^2 - k^4/q0^4)]、dx=1 波数约定、实部投影和整数步数，因此任何合法的替代离散化即使物理上正确，也无法实现预先计算的目标。The original spec describes the split-step scheme only qualitatively, omitting the explicit-Euler nonlinear update u_1 = u_N + dt(eps*u_N - u_N - u_N^3), the exact k-space propagator exp[dt(2k^2/q0^2 - k^4/q0^4)], the dx=1 wavenumber convention, the real-part projection, and the integer step count, so any legitimate alternative discretization fails the precomputed targets even if physically correct.

  • •

    55.2  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    规范将 Kx、Ky 和 Sk 命名为返回数组，但未指定网格网格方向（索引 = 'ij' 与 'xy' 转置结果）或 Sk = |fft2(u)|^2，无归一化，以 fftshift 为中心；选择其他约定的正确求解器无法与预先计算的目标进行逐元素比较。The spec names Kx, Ky, and Sk as returned arrays without specifying the meshgrid orientation (indexing=’ij’ vs ‘xy’ transposes the result) or that Sk = |fft2(u)|^2 with no normalization, fftshift-centered; a correct solver choosing the other convention fails element-wise comparison against the precomputed target.

  • •

    55.3  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    原始的峰值检测配方未指定箱数、范围、峰值查找方法和邻近容差（“一些合理的容差阈值”），从而使预先计算的目标无法重现；校正引脚 N//2 超过 [0, max k_r] 的等宽 bin、bin-center 报告、具有高度阈值的 scipy.signal.find_peaks 和 0.5*q0 邻近窗口。The original peak-detection recipe leaves the bin count, range, peak-finding method, and proximity tolerance (‘some reasonable tolerance threshold’) unspecified, making the precomputed target unreproducible; the correction pins N//2 equal-width bins over [0, max k_r], bin-center reporting, scipy.signal.find_peaks with a height threshold, and a 0.5*q0 proximity window.

  • •

    55.3  表面：交叉引用损坏surface: Broken cross-reference  ⋅\cdot中性清理neutral-cleanup
    该规范列出了peak_found_near_q0的“狭窄”标准，实际检测算法（仅接近度+高度）永远不会应用，并使用不一致的输出名称peak_near_q0_location而不是peak_location_near_q0；修正消除了幻像标准并对齐了标识符。The spec lists a ‘narrowness’ criterion for peak_found_near_q0 that the actual detection algorithm (proximity + height only) never applies, and uses the inconsistent output name peak_near_q0_location instead of peak_location_near_q0; the correction removes the phantom criterion and aligns the identifier.

  • •

    55.4  表面：交叉引用损坏surface: Broken cross-reference  ⋅\cdot中性清理neutral-cleanup
    “如果没有形成条带则设置为 0”子句被附加到 min_height 输入参数上，它是没有意义的（min_height 是调用者提供的阈值，而不是返回值）；更正将其移动到 stripe_mode 输出，在那里它正确地描述了无模式返回约定。The ‘set to 0 if no stripe is formed’ clause was attached to the min_height input parameter, where it is meaningless (min_height is a caller-supplied threshold, not a return value); the correction moves it to the stripe_mode output, where it correctly describes the no-pattern return convention.

  • •

    55.4  科学：过度严格的宽容scientific: Over-tight tolerance  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    该测试将包括混沌、RNG 和离散化敏感 u 字段以及原始 Sk 的完整输出元组与预先计算的位精确值进行比较；即使物理上有意义的可观察量（if_form_stripes 和 stripe_mode）匹配，任何使用合法方案变体的正确求解器都会失败，因此校正将全元组比较替换为 u/Sk 上的形状检查加上 stripe_mode 上的 allclose 和 if_form_stripes 上的相等性。The test compared the full output tuple including the chaotic, RNG- and discretization-sensitive u field and raw Sk to precomputed bit-exact values; any correct solver using a legitimate scheme variant fails even when the physically meaningful observables (if_form_stripes and stripe_mode) match, so the correction replaces full-tuple comparison with shape checks on u/Sk plus allclose on stripe_mode and equality on if_form_stripes.

问题 57 —Problem 57 —1D_harmonic_oscillator_numerov_shooting 物理学、计算物理学Physics, Computational Physics（3个缺陷）(3 defects)

原始来源：Original source: (Noumerov, 1924)

  • •

    57.2  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    原始规范省略了 Numerov 种子值 (u0 = u_b, u1 = u_b + step*up_b) 并且没有说明 step 是否带有其符号，因此任何使用不同种子或 abs(step) 的实现都会被黄金测试拒绝，尽管它是有效的 Numerov 积分器。The original spec omits the Numerov seed values (u0 = u_b, u1 = u_b + step*up_b) and does not say whether step carries its sign, so any implementation using a different seed or abs(step) is rejected by the gold tests despite being a valid Numerov integrator.

  • •

    57.4  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    原始规范说要计算连续元素之间的符号变化，但未定义零元素处理；黄金使用乘积 < 0 规则（零是符号中性的），因此将零视为符号翻转的实现会返回错误的节点计数并导致测试失败。The original spec says to count sign changes between consecutive elements but leaves zero-element handling undefined; the gold uses a product < 0 rule (zeros are sign-neutral), so an implementation treating a zero as a sign-flip returns the wrong node count and fails the test.

  • •

    57.5  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    该规范没有规定将束缚态能量作为节点数发生变化的第一个试验能量，没有进一步细化；黄金在能量网格上存储粗略的首次交叉值（例如 n=0 能量 ~1.0289，而不是精确的特征值 1.0），因此更准确的二分细化结果会被评级为错误。The spec does not state that bound-state energies are taken as the first trial energy where the node count changes, with no further refinement; the gold stores coarse first-crossing values on the energy grid (e.g. n=0 energy ~1.0289 rather than the exact eigenvalue 1.0), so a more accurate bisection-refined result is graded wrong.

问题 58 —Problem 58 —托尔曼_奥本海默_沃尔科夫_明星Tolman_Oppenheimer_Volkoff_star 物理学、天体物理学Physics, Astrophysics（6个缺陷）(6 defects)

    58.2  科学：错误的黄金（方法/符号/对称性）scientific: Wrong gold (method / sign / symmetry)  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    文档字符串将返回值命名为“eps：特定内能”，但函数 eos_rho_from_press 计算并返回密度，因此遵循文档字符串的求解器返回错误的物理量，并且每次测试都失败。The docstring names the return value ‘eps: the specific internal energy’ but the function eos_rho_from_press computes and returns density, so a docstring-following solver returns the wrong physical quantity and fails every test.

    58.3  表面：交叉引用损坏surface: Broken cross-reference  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong

  • •

    58.4  科学：错误的黄金（方法/符号/对称性）scientific: Wrong gold (method / sign / symmetry)  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    背景说明质量连续性 ODE 为 dm/dr = 4 pi r^3 mu，但正确的 GR 关系是 dm/dr = 4 pi r^2 mu；错误的 r^3 指数与黄金被积函数相矛盾，标志着物理上正确的实现是错误的。The background states the mass-continuity ODE as dm/dr = 4 pi r^3 mu, but the correct GR relation is dm/dr = 4 pi r^2 mu; the erroneous r^3 exponent contradicts the gold integrand and marks a physically correct implementation wrong.

  • •

    58.5  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    原始提示省略了重现黄金所需的约定：从 r=0 到 r_max 的均匀径向网格、参考规 phi(0)=0、表面规则（第一个半径，其中 P<=0）以及流逝公式 sqrt(1-2M/R)*exp(-phi(R))；如果没有这些，正确的方法求解器就无法匹配目标输出。The original prompt omits the conventions required to reproduce gold: the uniform radial grid from r=0 to r_max, the reference gauge phi(0)=0, the surface rule (first radius where P<=0), and the lapse formula sqrt(1-2M/R)*exp(-phi(R)); without these a correct-method solver cannot match the target output.

  • •

    58.5  科学：过度严格的宽容scientific: Over-tight tolerance  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    默认容差 np.allclose 有效地要求与一个积分器的轨迹完全一致，拒绝其他 ODE 求解器的正确解；此外，第一个测试的网格（npoints=200，rmax=100000）太粗糙且数值不稳定，无法产生良好解析的目标，因此容差和网格参数都会抑制正确的实现。Default-tolerance np.allclose effectively demands exact agreement with one integrator’s trajectory, rejecting correct solutions from other ODE solvers; additionally, the first test’s grid (npoints=200, rmax=100000) is too coarse and numerically unstable to yield a well-resolved target, so both the tolerance and grid parameters suppress correct implementations.

问题 59 —Problem 59 —车辆质量评估VQE 物理学、量子信息/计算Physics, Quantum Information/Computing（3个缺陷）(3 defects)

    59.1  表面：接口/退货合同不匹配surface: Interface / return-contract mismatch  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    脚手架的 return 语句引用“Rz”，这是一个从未为构建“R”的通用轴函数“rotation_matrices(axis, theta)”定义的 z 特定变量名称；遵循记录的输出变量的任何解决方案都会引发 NameError 或不返回任何内容。The scaffold’s return statement references ‘Rz‘, a z-specific variable name never defined for the general-axis function ‘rotation_matrices(axis, theta)‘, which builds ‘R‘; any solution following the documented output variable raises NameError or returns nothing.

  • •

    59.2  科学：非歧视性测试（物理）scientific: Non-discriminating test (physics)  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    该测试将复杂内积 vdot(ansatz_o, ansatz_c) 直接与范数的实际乘积进行比较；物理上有效的 ansatz 与全局相位不同 e^(i*phi) 满足大小上的柯西-施瓦茨等式，但不满足实部/虚部，因此 np.isclose 失败并且正确的状态被拒绝。该修复在内积上使用 np.abs 来实现相位不变性，并添加显式单位范数检查。The test compares the complex inner product vdot(ansatz_o, ansatz_c) directly against the real product of norms; a physically valid ansatz differing by a global phase e^(i*phi) satisfies the Cauchy-Schwarz equality in magnitude but not in real/imaginary parts, so np.isclose fails and the correct state is rejected. The fix uses np.abs on the inner product for phase invariance and adds an explicit unit-norm check.

  • •

    59.3  科学：非歧视性测试（物理）scientific: Non-discriminating test (physics)  ⋅\cdot中性清理neutral-cleanup
    原始的第三个测试用例 (U = CNOT21 * (H S_dag x H S_dag), psi = |1,-1j> x |1,-1j>/2) 给出与早期案例一致的 Z1 期望，并且没有添加新的覆盖范围；它被替换为 U = I, psi = |10>，这将 Z1 = -1（状态 |1> 中的第一个量子位）作为不同的分支。正确的实现可以通过两种形式，因此这是测试覆盖率的重新设计，对通过/失败方向没有影响。The original third test case (U = CNOT21 * (H S_dag x H S_dag), psi = |1,-1j> x |1,-1j>/2) gives a Z1 expectation coinciding with earlier cases and adds no new coverage; it is replaced with U = I, psi = |10>, which exercises Z1 = -1 (first qubit in state |1>) as a distinct branch. A correct implementation passes both forms, so this is a test-coverage redesign with no effect on pass/fail direction.

    60.2  科学：错误的黄金（方法/符号/对称性）scientific: Wrong gold (method / sign / symmetry)  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong

    62.1  表面：接口/退货合同不匹配surface: Interface / return-contract mismatch  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    脚手架仅定义了“class EnlargedBlock”，其“__init__”和“print_all”方法重复，而“class Block”（单站点块所需）完全不存在，使得提供的标头在语法和逻辑上不可用。The scaffold defined only ‘class EnlargedBlock‘ with its ‘__init__‘ and ‘print_all‘ methods duplicated, while ‘class Block‘ (required for single-site blocks) was entirely absent, making the provided header syntactically and logically unusable.

    62.3  表面：交叉引用损坏surface: Broken cross-reference  ⋅\cdot中性清理neutral-cleanup
    问题文本中的三个交叉引用留空（“来自步骤”、“在步骤中定义”、“如步骤中所述”），迫使求解器猜测哪些先前步骤定义了所需的运算符和哈密顿量；更正填写正确的步骤编号（62.2、62.4）。Three cross-references in the problem text were left blank (“from step ,”, “defined in step”, “as described in step .”), forcing solvers to guess which prior steps define the required operators and Hamiltonian; the correction fills in the correct step numbers (62.2, 62.4).

    62.5  科学：非歧视性测试（物理）scientific: Non-discriminating test (physics)  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    DMRG 截断步骤对简并 SU(2) 多重态进行操作，因此简并子空间内的特征基仅定义为任意正交旋转；比较原始运算符矩阵条目会拒绝恰好使用不同有效量规的正确实现。该修复改为检查基不变量（H 的特征值、conn_Sz 和 conn_Sp 的奇异值）。The DMRG truncation step operates on a degenerate SU(2) multiplet, so the eigenbasis within the degenerate subspace is defined only up to an arbitrary orthogonal rotation; comparing raw operator-matrix entries rejects correct implementations that happen to use a different valid gauge. The fix checks basis-invariant quantities (eigenvalues of H, singular values of conn_Sz and conn_Sp) instead.

  • •

    62.6  表面：轻微破损测试surface: Trivially broken test  ⋅\cdot中性清理neutral-cleanup
    步骤 62.6 的测试套件列出了两次 run_dmrg(block, 100, 100, model_d) 调用 - 一次作为情况 3，再次作为尾随情况 6 - 生成没有明显参数变化的冗余重复条目。修正后的套件保留了五个独特的测试用例（m0 和 m 不同）并删除了重复的用例，消除了不必要的冗余，而无需更改解决方案通过或失败的情况。The test suite for step 62.6 listed the run_dmrg(block, 100, 100, model_d) call twice—once as case 3 and again as a trailing case 6—producing a redundant duplicate entry with no distinct parameter variation. The corrected suite retains five unique test cases (varying m0 and m) and drops the repeated case, eliminating unnecessary redundancy without changing which solutions pass or fail.

问题 63 —Problem 63 —估计股票期权价格Estimating_Stock_Option_Price 数学、计算金融Mathematics, Computational Finance（7个缺陷）(7 defects)

原始来源：Original source: (Schwartz, 1977)

  • •

    63.2  科学：错误的黄金（方法/符号/对称性）scientific: Wrong gold (method / sign / symmetry)  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    背景方程给出的到期收益为 V(t_max, p) = max(K-s, 0)，即欧式看跌期权收益，但问题对欧式看涨期权定价，其正确的终止条件为 V(t_max, p) = max(s-K, 0)。边界条件中的符号反转会通过任何时间向后 PDE 求解器传播，产生对应于看跌期权而非看涨期权的选项值。使用调用支付 max(s-K, 0) 的正确实现无法重现后台指定的错误边界，因此任何此类解决方案都会被评为不正确。The background equation gives the expiry payoff as V(t_max, p) = max(K-s, 0), which is the European put payoff, but the problem prices a European call whose correct terminal condition is V(t_max, p) = max(s-K, 0). This sign reversal in the boundary condition propagates through any backward-in-time PDE solver, producing option values that correspond to a put rather than a call. A correct implementation using the call payoff max(s-K, 0) cannot reproduce the wrong boundary specified in the background, so any such solution is graded as incorrect.

  • •

    63.3  科学：错误的黄金（方法/符号/对称性）scientific: Wrong gold (method / sign / symmetry)  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    显式有限差分更新公式将 c 系数项写为 c*V^n_{j-1}，复制左邻索引；标准三点中心差模板需要 c*V^n_{j+1}。如果没有右邻居，则递归 V^{n+1}_j = a*V^n_{j-1} + b*V^n_j + c*V^n_{j-1} 与上面所述的模板是自相矛盾的。从该公式生成的任何参考解决方案或黄金目标都会在每个时间步传播错误的值，因此预期方案的正确实现会产生不同的数值输出并使比较失败。The explicit finite-difference update formula writes the c-coefficient term as c*V^n_{j-1}, duplicating the left-neighbour index; the standard three-point central-difference stencil requires c*V^n_{j+1}. With the right neighbour absent the recurrence V^{n+1}_j = a*V^n_{j-1} + b*V^n_j + c*V^n_{j-1} is self-contradictory with the stencil stated just above it. Any reference solution or gold target generated from this formula propagates the wrong value at every time step, so a correct implementation of the intended scheme produces different numerical output and fails the comparison.

  • •

    63.4  科学：错误的黄金（方法/符号/对称性）scientific: Wrong gold (method / sign / symmetry)  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    文档字符串将三对角矩阵 D 标记为形状 (N_t-2)x(N_t-2)，但 D 对内部价格节点进行操作，使其正确的形状为 (N_p-2)x(N_p-2)；错误的维度标签与步骤 63.3 自己的描述相矛盾，并误导任何求解器的实现。The docstring labels the tridiagonal matrix D as shape (N_t-2)x(N_t-2), but D operates on interior price nodes, making its correct shape (N_p-2)x(N_p-2); the wrong dimension label contradicts step 63.3’s own description and misdirects any solver implementation.

  • •

    63.1  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    输出描述仅表示“p_min 和 p_max 之间的 np.linspace”，而未指定网格采用对数价格或边界必须保持给定（降序）顺序；测试通过了已经交换的边界，因此升序的 linspace 会生成不同的数组，并且无法正确实现。The output description says only ‘np.linspace between p_min and p_max’ without specifying that the grid is in log-price or that the bounds must stay in the given (descending) order; the test passes bounds already swapped, so an ascending linspace produces a different array and fails a correct implementation.

  • •

    63.6  科学：非歧视性测试（物理）scientific: Non-discriminating test (physics)  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    测试设置 S0=100，min_price=200（= 行使价/5），将初始股票价格置于网格下限以下；在不跨越该点的对数价格网格上对 log(S0) 处的 V 进行插值是未定义的，这使得测试不适定。该修复将 S0=400 移入范围内，并指定楼层时间索引和对数网格线性插值约定。The test sets S0=100 with min_price=200 (= strike/5), placing the initial stock price below the grid’s lower bound; interpolating V at log(S0) over a log-price grid that does not span that point is undefined, making the test ill-posed. The fix moves S0=400 in-range and specifies the floor time-index and log-grid linear-interpolation convention.

  • •

    63.3  表面：接口/退货合同不匹配surface: Interface / return-contract mismatch  ⋅\cdot中性清理neutral-cleanup
    步骤 63.3 和 63.4 的原始文档字符串没有声明 D 是 scipy.sparse 矩阵，从而使跨步骤类型契约隐式存在；由于黄金实现返回并消耗稀疏矩阵，因此遗漏可能会导致下游步骤中的静默类型不匹配。The original docstrings for steps 63.3 and 63.4 do not state that D is a scipy.sparse matrix, leaving the cross-step type contract implicit; since the gold implementation returns and consumes a sparse matrix, the omission can cause silent type mismatches in downstream steps.

  • •

    63.2  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    原始提示（“正确地设置边界条件……关于时间和价格的二维数组”）没有指定哪个轴对应于价格与时间、支付和边界行中使用的对数价格形式 s=e^p 或角优先顺序；具有转置布局或不同角分配的正确解决方案将被确定性测试错误地拒绝。The original prompt (‘correctly puts boundary condition … 2D array w.r.t. time and price’) does not specify which axis corresponds to price versus time, the log-price form s=e^p used in the payoff and boundary rows, or the corner-precedence order; a correct solution with a transposed layout or different corner assignment would be wrongly rejected by the deterministic test.

问题 64 —Problem 64 —GCMC 材料科学、分子模拟Material Science, Molecular Modeling（5个缺陷）(5 defects)

    64.2  表面：轻微破损测试surface: Trivially broken test  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    dist() 函数返回单个浮点数，但测试用 [0] 对其进行索引；正确的标量返回不能以这种方式下标，因此断言将崩溃或默默地与正确的解决方案进行错误比较。The dist() function returns a single float, but the test indexed it with [0]; a correct scalar return cannot be subscripted this way, so the assertion would crash or silently mis-compare against a correct solution.

  • •

    64.4  表面：接口/退货合同不匹配surface: Interface / return-contract mismatch  ⋅\cdot中性清理neutral-cleanup

  • •

    64.4  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    当两个粒子在最小图像约定下重合时，伦纳德-琼斯能量发散；不同的正确实现可能会返回 +inf 或 nan，因此原始未指定的约定可能会导致正确的解决方案无法进行黄金比较。When two particles coincide under the minimum-image convention, the Lennard-Jones energy diverges; different correct implementations may return +inf or nan, so the original unspecified convention could cause a correct solution to fail the gold comparison.

  • •

    64.6  科学：规格scientific: Spec↔\leftrightarrow黄金矛盾gold contradiction  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    该规范给出了真实的 SI 氩常数（质量以千克为单位，西格玛以米为单位），并附有“使 J 和 s 无量纲”的不连贯指令，而测试用例以 Lennard-Jones 简化单位运行（epsilon = sigma = m = 1，k_B = 1）；自相矛盾的规格迫使黄金拒绝做出不正确的尺寸选择。The spec gave real SI argon constants (mass in kg, sigma in meters) alongside an incoherent instruction to ‘make J and s dimensionless’, while the test cases run in Lennard-Jones reduced units (epsilon = sigma = m = 1, k_B = 1); the contradictory spec forced incorrect dimensional choices that the gold rejects.

  • •

    64.6  科学：过度严格的宽容scientific: Over-tight tolerance  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    有限蒙特卡罗运行（1e5 步骤）产生具有固有随机方差的平衡粒子计数平均值； 1% 的相对容差太严格，无法可靠地接受正确的 GCMC 实施，而 5% 的相对容差可以容纳统计波动，但仍会拒绝不正确的结果。A finite Monte Carlo run (1e5 steps) produces an equilibrium particle-count average with inherent stochastic variance; a 1% relative tolerance is too tight to reliably accept a correct GCMC implementation, whereas 5% accommodates statistical fluctuations while still rejecting incorrect results.

    65.1  表面：接口/退货合同不匹配surface: Interface / return-contract mismatch  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong

    65.1  表面：接口/退货合同不匹配surface: Interface / return-contract mismatch  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong

  • •

    65.2, 65.3, 65.6  表面：接口/退货合同不匹配surface: Interface / return-contract mismatch  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong

    65.3, 65.6  表面：交叉引用损坏surface: Broken cross-reference  ⋅\cdot中性清理neutral-cleanup

    65.4  科学：错误的黄金（方法/符号/对称性）scientific: Wrong gold (method / sign / symmetry)  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    原始公式 rho” = V rho' V^dagger 对于两方状态在量纲上是不可能的：V 是 2^n x 2，因此两方酉必须是 VV 并且伴随作用在左边；正确的公式是 rho” = (VV)^dagger rho' (VV)，并且任何正确的实现都会根据破坏的规范被评级为错误。The original formula rho” = V rho’ V^dagger is dimensionally impossible for a two-party state: V is 2^n x 2, so the two-party unitary must be VV and the adjoint acts on the left; the correct formula is rho” = (VV)^dagger rho’ (VV), and any correct implementation of this is graded wrong against the broken spec.
