    67.5（步骤描述提示）67.5 (step_description_prompt)  科学：错误的黄金（方法/符号/对称性）scientific: Wrong gold (method / sign / symmetry)  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    步骤描述将物理常数标记为 hbar/m_e，但 hbar/m_e 包含速度乘以长度 (J.s / kg = m^2/s) 的量纲，这在量纲上与所述单位 meV nm^2 （能量乘以面积）不一致。引用的数值 76.2 meV nm^2 与 hbar^2/m_e 一致，而不是 hbar/m_e，并且测试用例通过使用 7.62 meV nm^2 / m_eff（hbar^2/m_e 的有效质量缩放）进行费米能量和速度计算来证实这一点。任何从字面上读取规范并将常数定义为 hbar/m_e 的解决方案都会以错误的物理尺寸进行操作，从而产生无法匹配预期输出的不正确的费米能量。The step description labels the physical constant as hbar/m_e, but hbar/m_e carries dimensions of velocity times length (J.s / kg = m^2/s), which is dimensionally inconsistent with the stated units meV nm^2 (energy times area). The quoted numerical value 76.2 meV nm^2 is consistent with hbar^2/m_e, not hbar/m_e, and the test cases confirm this by using 7.62 meV nm^2 / m_eff (the effective-mass scaling of hbar^2/m_e) for Fermi energy and velocity calculations. Any solution that reads the spec literally and defines the constant as hbar/m_e operates with a quantity of the wrong physical dimension, producing incorrect Fermi energies that cannot match the expected outputs.

  • •

    67.6（测试用例和一般测试）67.6 (test_cases and general_tests)  科学：过度严格的宽容scientific: Over-tight tolerance  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    该测试将 N=101 有限矩阵 RPA 数值结果与使用 1e-15 相对容差的封闭式分析表达式进行比较，这实际上是位精确的并且物理上无法实现：这种大小的有限差分结构会累积 O(N * eps_machine) 舍入误差，使合法结果接近 1e-13 相对误差或更大。同时，np.allclose 检查依赖于默认容差（rtol=1e-5、atol=1e-8），而没有明确的规范，使得跨环境的验收标准变得脆弱。将分析结果与任何物理上有意义的精度相匹配的正确解决方案将被拒绝。该修复将数字与分析的相对容差放宽至 1e-8，并向 allclose 调用添加显式 atol=rtol=1e-10。The test compares an N=101 finite-matrix RPA numerical result against a closed-form analytic expression using a relative tolerance of 1e-15, which is effectively bit-exact and physically unachievable: a finite-difference construction of this size accumulates O(N * eps_machine) rounding error, placing legitimate results near 1e-13 relative error or larger. Simultaneously, the np.allclose checks rely on default tolerances (rtol=1e-5, atol=1e-8) without explicit specification, making acceptance criteria brittle across environments. A correct solution matching the analytic result to any physically meaningful precision is rejected. The fix loosens the numeric-vs-analytic relative tolerance to 1e-8 and adds explicit atol=rtol=1e-10 to the allclose calls.

问题 68 —Problem 68 —氦原子dmchelium_atom_dmc 化学、量子化学Chemistry, Quantum Chemistry（5个缺陷）(5 defects)

原始来源：Original source: (Reynolds et al., 1982)

  • •

    68.3  科学：过度严格的宽容scientific: Over-tight tolerance  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    拉普拉斯测试断言 np.allclose(RMSE, precompulated_target)，将有限差分 RMSE 固定为一个精确的数值；任何具有稍微不同的舍入路径的正确实现都会失败。该修复将其替换为具有物理意义的合同，即数值与分析 RMSE 较小 (< 1e-2)。The Laplacian tests assert np.allclose(RMSE, precomputed_target), pinning the finite-difference RMSE to one exact numerical value; any correct implementation with a slightly different rounding path fails. The fix replaces this with the physically meaningful contract that the numerical-vs-analytic RMSE is small (< 1e-2).

  • •

    68.5  科学：取决于 RNG 的分级scientific: RNG-dependent grading  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    Metropolis 测试断言与种子最终步行器配置完全一致，需要按位复制特定的 RNG 轨迹；由于 Metropolis 平衡到 |psi|^2 与路径无关，唯一物理上正确的契约是平均半径收敛到已知的固定值 3/(2 alpha)。该修复将精确位置测试替换为统计平衡检查，并将函数重构为单次扫描，以便调用者控制循环。The Metropolis test asserted exact agreement with a seeded final walker configuration, requiring bitwise reproduction of a specific RNG trajectory; since Metropolis equilibration to |psi|^2 is path-irrelevant, the only physically correct contract is convergence of the mean radius to the known stationary value 3/(2 alpha). The fix replaces the exact-position test with that statistical equilibration check and refactors the function to a single sweep so callers control the loop.

  • •

    68.6  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    文档字符串使drift_new的标准化不明确（drift_old明确指出“乘以dtau”，但drift_new没有）并且没有给出返回值的规范，因此求解器可以合法地返回修剪的min（1，ratio）接受概率并无法通过黄金测试。该修复遵循这样的约定：drift_new 也乘以 dtau，并且需要原始的无上限 Metropolis-Hastings 比率。The docstring left drift_new’s normalization ambiguous (drift_old explicitly stated ‘multiplied by dtau’ but drift_new did not) and gave no specification for the return value, so a solver could legitimately return the clipped min(1, ratio) acceptance probability and fail the gold test. The fix pins the convention that drift_new is also multiplied by dtau and requires the raw uncapped Metropolis-Hastings ratio.

  • •

    68.7  科学：取决于 RNG 的分级scientific: RNG-dependent grading  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    最初的分支测试断言来自种子 np.random.multinomial 抽签的精确步行者指数，这隐含地要求一个特定的 RNG 协议；任何正确的替代方案（例如系统或残差重采样）都会产生不同的指数，并且尽管满足相同的物理契约，但仍会失败。扩散蒙特卡罗分支的真正要求只是walker i的期望重数与w_i /mean(w)成正比；种子锁定索引检查是按位复制工件，而不是物理正确性标准。该修复将精确索引断言替换为使用故意分布权重对 M=20000 次抽签进行的统计频率测试，因此任何无偏重采样方案都会通过，而退化方案（均匀采样，w^2 加权）会正确失败。The original branching test asserts exact walker indices from a seeded np.random.multinomial draw, which implicitly mandates one specific RNG protocol; any correct alternative such as systematic or residual resampling produces different indices and fails despite satisfying the same physical contract. The true requirement for Diffusion Monte Carlo branching is only that the expected multiplicity of walker i is proportional to w_i / mean(w); the seed-locked index check is a bitwise-reproduction artifact, not a physical correctness criterion. The fix replaces the exact-index assertion with a statistical frequency test over M=20000 draws using deliberately spread weights, so any unbiased resampling scheme passes while degenerate schemes (uniform sampling, w^2 weighting) correctly fail.

  • •

    68.8  科学：取决于 RNG 的分级scientific: RNG-dependent grading  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    四个原始测试在 run_dmc 返回的完整能量序列上播种 RNG 并断言 np.allclose，固定取决于分支选择顺序和预热细节的特定 RNG 轨迹 - 与扩散蒙特卡罗物理上无关的实现选择。任何使用不同分支方案、步行器排序或预热策略的正确 DMC 实现都会产生不同的随机轨迹，并且无法通过精确序列检查，即使其混合估计器正确收敛到氦基态能量 (-2.903724 Ha)。该修复用统计测试取代了轨迹固定断言：预热下降的平均局部能量必须落在 -2.903724 Ha 的 0.05 Ha 范围内，在种子之间验证稳健（|dev| ~ 0.008—0.010，dev + 3*SEM <= 0.022），并且重写提示和返回规范以说明混合估计器合同，而不是规定 RNG 协议详细信息。The four original tests seed the RNG and assert np.allclose on the full energy sequence returned by run_dmc, pinning a specific RNG trajectory that depends on branch-selection order and warm-up details—implementation choices that are physically irrelevant to diffusion Monte Carlo. Any correct DMC implementation that uses a different branching scheme, walker ordering, or warm-up strategy will produce a different stochastic trajectory and fail the exact-sequence check even though its mixed estimator converges correctly to the helium ground-state energy (-2.903724 Ha). The fix replaces the trajectory-pinning assertions with a statistical test: the warm-up-dropped mean local energy must fall within 0.05 Ha of -2.903724 Ha, validated robust across seeds (|dev| ~ 0.008—0.010, dev + 3*SEM <= 0.022), and the prompt and return specification are rewritten to state the mixed-estimator contract instead of prescribing RNG-protocol details.

    71.9  科学：错误的黄金（方法/符号/对称性）scientific: Wrong gold (method / sign / symmetry)  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong

问题 72 —Problem 72 —模型ising_model 物理学、凝聚态物理学Physics, Condensed Matter Physics（10 个缺陷）(10 defects)

  • •

    72.1  表面：错别字/格式surface: Typo / formatting  ⋅\cdot中性清理neutral-cleanup
    将记录的邻居顺序（左、上、右、下）重新排序为（上、右、下、左）；由于所有四个邻居都被求和，所以这种变化只是表面性的，不会改变黄金或测试行为。The documented neighbor order (left, above, right, below) is reordered to (above, right, below, left); since all four neighbors are summed, the change is cosmetic and leaves no gold or test behavior altered.

  • •

    72.8  科学：取决于 RNG 的分级scientific: RNG-dependent grading  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    原始测试需要在固定种子下精确地按温度 mag2 阵列进行复制，这对于任何与 RNG 绘制顺序不同的正确实现都失败了；替换使用足够的平衡扫描来验证物理行为（低 T 下的高磁化强度、高 T 下的低磁化强度、单调间隙超过 0.5）。The original tests require exact per-temperature mag2 array reproduction under a fixed seed, which fails for any correct implementation that differs in RNG draw order; the replacement validates physics behavior (high magnetization at low T, low magnetization at high T, monotone gap exceeding 0.5) using sufficient equilibration sweeps.

  • •

    72.9  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    原始规范说返回“导数最小化的温度”，但离散导数 np.diff(mag2)/np.diff(T) 是按间隔定义的，而不是单个温度，因此该指令对于是否返回左端点或右端点不明确；修正将约定固定为 T_list[argmin(…)]（最速下降区间的左端点）。The original spec says to return ‘the temperature at which the derivative is minimized,’ but the discrete derivative np.diff(mag2)/np.diff(T) is defined on intervals rather than single temperatures, so the instruction is ambiguous about whether to return the left or right endpoint; the correction pins the convention as T_list[argmin(…)] (the left endpoint of the steepest-drop interval).

  • •

    72.9  科学：取决于 RNG 的分级scientific: RNG-dependent grading  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    原始测试使用 np.random.seed(0) 和一个小网格 (N=5, nsweeps=100) 调用 scan_T，然后断言 np.allclose(calc_transition(Ts, mag2), target)：预先计算的目标编码特定的 MC RNG 轨迹，而不是导数 argmin 估计器的数学正确性。任何使用不同 RNG 状态、扫描计数或 Metropolis 排序的正确实现都会产生不同的 mag2 数组，并且即使正确识别临界温度，也会导致 allclose 比较失败。该修复将这些 RNG 耦合测试替换为合成输入上的确定性单元测试（T=[1, 2, 3, 4] 处的 [1.0, 1.0, 0.2, 0.1]，其中离散导数的 argmin 明确为 T=2.0），并且物理锚断言恢复的 Tc 位于精确 2D Ising 值 2/ln(1+sqrt(2)) 的 0.2 范围内= 2.26919。The original tests call scan_T with np.random.seed(0) and a small lattice (N=5, nsweeps=100), then assert np.allclose(calc_transition(Ts, mag2), target): the precomputed target encodes a specific MC RNG trajectory, not the mathematical correctness of the derivative-argmin estimator. Any correct implementation that uses a different RNG state, sweep count, or Metropolis ordering produces a different mag2 array and fails the allclose comparison even though it correctly identifies the critical temperature. The fix replaces these RNG-coupled tests with a deterministic unit test on synthetic input ([1.0, 1.0, 0.2, 0.1] at T=[1, 2, 3, 4], where the argmin of the discrete derivative is unambiguously T=2.0) and a physics anchor asserting the recovered Tc lies within 0.2 of the exact 2D Ising value 2/ln(1+sqrt(2)) = 2.26919.

    77.5（f_ij 函数头）77.5 (f_ij function_header)  表面：接口/退货合同不匹配surface: Interface / return-contract mismatch  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong

    77.6（E_tail步骤_背景）77.6 (E_tail step_background)  科学：错误的黄金（方法/符号/对称性）scientific: Wrong gold (method / sign / symmetry)  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    步骤背景中的 Lennard-Jones 能量尾公式省略了所需的 1/V (V = L^3) 归一化因子：正确的总尾部校正是 (8/3) pi N^2 eps sigma^3 […] / V，但原始版本中没有 /V 并将其错误标记为“每个粒子”。由于 N^2 前因子已经计算了所有对相互作用，因此除以 V 会将扩展总和转换为密集批量校正；省略它会使结果膨胀 V 倍（对于典型的 10^3 模拟盒来说，该倍数为 1000）。因此，任何包含 1/V 因子的正确实现都会产生与预先计算的 gold 相差三个数量级的值，并且会被错误地拒绝。The Lennard-Jones energy-tail formula in the step background omits the required 1/V (V = L^3) normalization factor: the correct total tail correction is (8/3) pi N^2 eps sigma^3 […] / V, but the original writes it without the /V and mislabels it “per particle.” Because the N^2 prefactor already counts all pair interactions, dividing by V converts the extensive sum to an intensive bulk correction; omitting it inflates the result by a factor of V (a factor of 1000 for a typical 10^3 simulation box). Any correct implementation that includes the 1/V factor therefore produces a value differing from the precomputed gold by three orders of magnitude and is incorrectly rejected.

  • •

    77.7（P_tail步骤_背景）77.7 (P_tail step_background)  科学：错误的黄金（方法/符号/对称性）scientific: Wrong gold (method / sign / symmetry)  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    Lennard-Jones 流体的长程压力尾部修正为 P_tail = (16/3) * pi * (N^2/V^2) * epsilon * sigma^3 * [(2/3)(sigma/r_c)^9 - (sigma/r_c)^3]，其中 N^2/V^2 因子反映尾部体积上积分对密度的平方。原始公式完全省略了 1/V^2 (V = L^3) 因子，使得预先计算的 gold 太大了 V^2 倍。因此，任何包含 1/V^2 的正确实现都会产生与典型模拟盒尺寸的黄金相差几个数量级的压力，从而导致该步骤失败，无论解决方案的物理正确性如何。The long-range pressure tail correction for a Lennard-Jones fluid is P_tail = (16/3) * pi * (N^2/V^2) * epsilon * sigma^3 * [(2/3)(sigma/r_c)^9 - (sigma/r_c)^3], where the N^2/V^2 factor reflects the pair density squared integrated over the tail volume. The original formula omitted the 1/V^2 (V = L^3) factor entirely, making the precomputed gold too large by a factor of V^2. Any correct implementation that includes 1/V^2 therefore produces a pressure differing from the gold by orders of magnitude for typical simulation box sizes, causing the step to fail regardless of the solution’s physical correctness.

  • •

    77.7（P_尾函数_标头）77.7 (P_tail function_header)  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    P_tail 必须以 bar 为单位返回压力，但从 zeptojoules/nm^3 到 bar 的单位转换取决于 L、sigma 和 rc 的单位为纳米，epsilon 的单位为 zeptojoule；从文档字符串中省略这些单位会使实现无法导出 x10 转换因子。P_tail must return pressure in bar, but the unit conversion from zeptojoules per nm^3 to bar depends on knowing that L, sigma, and rc are in nanometers and epsilon is in zeptojoules; omitting these units from the docstring leaves implementations unable to derive the x10 conversion factor.

  • •

    77.9（温度测试用例）77.9 (temperature test_cases)  表面：轻微破损测试surface: Trivially broken test  ⋅\cdot中性清理neutral-cleanup
    N=1 温度测试通过一维 shape-(3,) 速度数组，其中函数的契约为 shape-(N,3)；该修复将其重塑为 (1,3) 以保证合约一致性，尽管数值结果没有变化，因为 np.sum(v**2) 在这里是形状不变的。The N=1 temperature test passes a 1D shape-(3,) velocity array where the function’s contract is shape-(N,3); the fix reshapes it to (1,3) for contract consistency, though the numerical result is unchanged since np.sum(v**2) is shape-invariant here.

  • •

    77.10（压力步骤_背景）77.10 (pressure step_background)  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    维里压力公式将 r_ij 定义为“从粒子 i 到 j 的位移”(= r_j - r_i)，但正确的维里约定要求 r_ij = r_i - r_j；使用错误的符号会翻转维里压力的符号，将正确的 +8.851 结果变成 -8.851。The virial-pressure formula defines r_ij as ‘displacement from particle i to j’ (= r_j - r_i), but the correct virial convention requires r_ij = r_i - r_j; using the wrong sign flips the sign of the virial pressure, turning a correct +8.851 result into -8.851.

  • •

    77.10（压力测试用例）和77.11（强制测试用例）77.10 (pressure test_cases) and 77.11 (forces test_cases)  表面：轻微破损测试surface: Trivially broken test  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    在压力 (77.10) 和力 (77.11) 步骤的第二个测试用例中，N 被声明为 2，但位置数组有三行，使得测试定义不明确；该修复删除了虚假的第三行，因此数组形状与 N 匹配。In the second test case of both the pressure (77.10) and forces (77.11) steps, N is declared as 2 but the positions array has three rows, making the test ill-defined; the fix removes the spurious third row so the array shape matches N.

问题 79 —Problem 79 —Nose_Hoover_chain_thermostat 材料科学、分子模拟Material Science, Molecular Modeling（8个缺陷）(8 defects)

原始来源：Original source: (Martyna et al., 1996)

  • •

    Problem_io / sub_steps[3]（步骤 79.4） function_headerproblem_io / sub_steps[3] (step 79.4) function_header  表面：接口/退货合同不匹配surface: Interface / return-contract mismatch  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    规范将位置和速度轨迹声明为形状 (nsteps, 1)，但黄金比较期望形状为 (nsteps,) 的一维数组；遵循指定形状的求解器实际上会产生二维输出，并被错误地拒绝。The spec declared position and velocity trajectories as shape (nsteps, 1), but the gold comparison expects 1-D arrays of shape (nsteps,); a solver following the stated shape literally produces a 2-D output and is wrongly rejected.

  • •

    sub_steps[3]（步骤 79.4） function_headersub_steps[3] (step 79.4) function_header  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    原始标头没有给出轨迹索引的约定，导致第一个条目是初始状态还是一步后的状态不明确；修正遵循先记录后步约定（x[0]=x0，v[0]=v0），这是匹配黄金轨迹的承载。The original header gave no convention for trajectory indexing, leaving ambiguous whether the first entry is the initial state or the state after one step; the correction pins the record-then-step convention (x[0]=x0, v[0]=v0), which is load-bearing for matching the gold trajectory.

  • •

    sub_steps[1]（步骤 79.2）和 sub_steps[2]（步骤 79.3）sub_steps[1] (step 79.2) and sub_steps[2] (step 79.3) function_header  表面：接口/退货合同不匹配surface: Interface / return-contract mismatch  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    长度为 M 的 Nose-Hoover 链具有每个链节的力 G_i、恒温器速度 v_xi_i 和位置 xi_i，每个向量的形状为 (M,)；将它们声明为标量浮点数与多链接算法相矛盾，并导致正确的基于数组的实现被拒绝。The Nose-Hoover chain of length M has per-link forces G_i, thermostat velocities v_xi_i, and positions xi_i, each a vector of shape (M,); declaring them as scalar float contradicts the multi-link algorithm and causes a correct array-based implementation to be rejected.

  • •

    sub_steps[1]（步骤 79.2）和 sub_steps[2]（步骤 79.3）sub_steps[1] (step 79.2) and sub_steps[2] (step 79.3) function_header  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    原始文档字符串仅将 dt 描述为“积分时间步长”，隐藏了 nhc_step 将链推进了其参数的一半；遵循刘维尔框架 (exp(iL w_j dt/2)) 的求解器传递 w_j*dt/2 而不是 w_j*dt，从而在积分时间中产生 2 倍误差。The original docstring described dt only as ‘the integration time step’, concealing that nhc_step advances the chain by half its argument; solvers following the Liouville framing (exp(iL w_j dt/2)) passed w_j*dt/2 instead of w_j*dt, producing a factor-of-2 error in integration time.

  • •

    sub_steps[1]（步骤 79.2）step_description_promptsub_steps[1] (step 79.2) step_description_prompt  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    提示在G_1/G_k公式中使用了k_B和Q_k，没有固定它们的数值；多个选择（例如 Q=1 或 k_B=2）都会导致目标失败，而只有 k_B=1 和 Q_k=k_B*T/omega^2 才能重现黄金，如果没有这些约定，则该步骤不适定。The prompt used k_B and Q_k in the G_1/G_k formulas without fixing their numerical values; multiple choices (e.g. Q=1 or k_B=2) each fail the targets, while only k_B=1 and Q_k=k_B*T/omega^2 reproduce the gold, making the step ill-posed without these conventions.

  • •

    sub_steps[2]（步骤 79.3）step_description_promptsub_steps[2] (step 79.3) step_description_prompt  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    命名“吉田四阶方法”是不够的，因为存在多个具有不同系数的四阶合成方案；如果不固定三级跳权重 w1=1/(2-2^(1/3))、w2=1-2*w1，则黄金轨迹是不明确的，并且使用不同有效方案的正确实现会被错误地拒绝。Naming ‘Yoshida’s fourth-order method’ is insufficient because multiple 4th-order composition schemes exist with different coefficients; without pinning the triple-jump weights w1=1/(2-2^(1/3)), w2=1-2*w1, the gold trajectory is ambiguous and correct implementations using a different valid scheme are wrongly rejected.

  • •

    sub_steps[0]（步骤 79.1） test_casessub_steps[0] (step 79.1) test_cases  科学：非歧视性测试（物理）scientific: Non-discriminating test (physics)  ⋅\cdot太宽松（测试收紧）too-lenient (test tightened)
    所有三个原始 Verlet 测试用例均设置 x0=0.0，因此错误处理位置更新中初始位置项的实现无论如何都会通过；添加 x0=1.0 的情况可以锻炼位置依赖性并关闭此错误通过漏洞。All three original Verlet test cases set x0=0.0, so an implementation that mishandles the initial-position term in the position update passes regardless; adding a case with x0=1.0 exercises the position dependence and closes this false-pass hole.

  • •

    sub_steps[3]（步骤 79.4） test_cases/general_testssub_steps[3] (step 79.4) test_cases / general_tests  科学：过度严格的宽容scientific: Over-tight tolerance  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    Nose-Hoover 链 (NHC) 积分器是混乱的：对于 M=2 的链，使用 N=20000 和 N=40000 步计算的轨迹会在任何两个正确但位不相同的实现中累积呈指数发散的浮点舍入误差。最初的测试将完整轨迹逐元素与预先计算的黄金进行比较，因此中间浮点数因机器 epsilon 量而异的正确解算器在步数远远超出李雅普诺夫时间时被拒绝。将 M=2 的情况减少到 N=500 和 N=1000 可以保持系统内的集成，在该系统中，正确的实现在精确比较下仍然可以相互重现，从而消除错误故障，而不会削弱两个恒温器​​链的覆盖范围。The Nose-Hoover chain (NHC) integrator is chaotic: for M=2 chains, trajectories computed with N=20000 and N=40000 steps accumulate exponentially diverging floating-point rounding errors across any two correct-but-not-bit-identical implementations. The original test compared full trajectories element-wise against a precomputed gold, so a correct solver whose intermediate floats differ by machine-epsilon amounts is rejected at step counts far beyond the Lyapunov time. Reducing the M=2 cases to N=500 and N=1000 keeps the integration within the regime where correct implementations remain mutually reproducible under exact comparison, eliminating false failures without weakening coverage of the two-thermostat chain.

问题 80 —Problem 80 —安德森_恒温器Anderson_thermostat 材料科学、分子模拟Material Science, Molecular Modeling（5个缺陷）(5 defects)

原始来源：Original source: (Andersen, 1980)

  • •

    Problem_io/步骤 80.7（MD_NVT 函数_标头）problem_io / step 80.7 (MD_NVT function_header)  科学：规格scientific: Spec↔\leftrightarrow黄金矛盾gold contradiction  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    该规范描述了 Berendsen 恒温器加恒压器（NPT 系综），并声称返回修改后的盒子长度，这与问题的实际算法（Andersen NVT，无恒压器）和真正的五元素返回直接矛盾；遵循原始规范的求解器将实现错误的集成和错误的返回契约。The spec described a Berendsen thermostat plus barostat (NPT ensemble) and claimed a modified box length is returned, directly contradicting the problem’s actual algorithm (Andersen NVT, no barostat) and the true five-element return; a solver following the original spec would implement the wrong ensemble and wrong return contract.

  • •

    步骤 80.2 (E_ij) 和步骤 80.4 (f_ij) 函数头step 80.2 (E_ij) and step 80.4 (f_ij) function headers  表面：接口/退货合同不匹配surface: Interface / return-contract mismatch  ⋅\cdot中性清理neutral-cleanup
    E_ij 和 f_ij 文档字符串提到了截断并移动的 Yukawa 势作为可选组件，但任务中的任何位置都不存在 Yukawa 参数；这种虚假的提及会误导求解者，但不会影响评分，因为黄金测试仅锻炼 Lennard-Jones。The E_ij and f_ij docstrings mentioned a truncated-and-shifted Yukawa potential as an optional component, but no Yukawa parameters exist anywhere in the task; the spurious mention misleads solvers without affecting grading, since the gold tests only exercise Lennard-Jones.

  • •

    步骤 80.3（背景）、步骤 80.4（f_ij r 型）、步骤 80.5（强制返回）step 80.3 (background), step 80.4 (f_ij r-type), step 80.5 (forces Returns)  科学：未指定的约定scientific: Unspecified convention  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    背景将能量限制为“彼此相邻的两个原子”，而不是最小图像距离处的所有不同对（iThe background limited energy to ‘two atoms next to each other’ instead of all distinct pairs (i<j) at minimum-image distance; f_ij’s argument r was typed as a scalar float when the gold requires the signed 3D displacement vector r_j - r_i; and the Newton’s-third-law force accumulation sign convention was left implicit, so correct vectorial implementations could be graded wrong.

  • •

    步骤 80.5（强制）test_casesstep 80.5 (forces) test_cases  表面：轻微破损测试surface: Trivially broken test  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    forces() 测试声明 N=2，但提供了一个三行位置数组；迭代 N=2 的正确实现会默默地忽略第三个粒子，从而使测试输入自相矛盾，并且预期输出在物理上是错误的。The forces() test declared N=2 but supplied a three-row positions array; a correct implementation iterating over N=2 would silently ignore the third particle, making the test input self-contradictory and the expected output physically wrong.

  • •

    步骤 80.7 (MD_NVT) test_cases 和general_testsstep 80.7 (MD_NVT) test_cases and general_tests  科学：取决于 RNG 的分级scientific: RNG-dependent grading  ⋅\cdot太严格/错误too-strict/wrong
    N=200与initialize_fcc冲突，它返回6^3=216个格点，因此声明的粒子数与实际位置数组不匹配；此外，Andersen 恒温器进行随机碰撞绘制，因此如果没有固定的 RNG 种子，则温度和能量断言在运行中是不可重现的。N=200 conflicts with initialize_fcc, which returns 6^3=216 lattice sites, so the declared particle count mismatches the actual positions array; additionally, the Andersen thermostat makes stochastic collision draws, so without a fixed RNG seed the temperature and energy assertions are non-reproducible across runs.
